`CHANGELOG.md`:

```md
# 📝 Changelog

The app is at **g9**. Work on the repo, the installer and the `flashkit` command since then did not change the app's version, so it is listed together here.

## Since g9 (repo, installer and the `flashkit` command)
- Fixed a CI test that checked the README for its pre-publish placeholder text, so it failed on every repo that had actually been published.
- **Repo look:** new README with banner, badges, icons and a 3-step install; a user guide (`docs/GUIDE.md`); issue templates; a Material 3 Expressive author card with Chakradhar Tode branding.
- **FlashKit menu:** `flashkit` opens a menu - Start FlashKit, Run manually, Update, Check/install plugins, Doctor, autostart, Uninstall. `start flashkit` and `flashkit start` open the app directly, and there is a *FlashKit* entry for the Termux:Widget home-screen widget.
- **Uninstall:** `flashkit uninstall` removes FlashKit and asks what to delete; `-y` removes everything - the commands, settings, app files and the Termux:GUI / Termux:API apps.
- **Plugin installer:** finds installed plugins through root, never overwrites a working one, tries the next source if Android refuses a download, and has a no-root path (Android's own installer opens). With root it also allows "Display over other apps" for you.
- **After a restart:** if Android restarts Termux while a plugin installs, the next session opens the FlashKit menu once by itself.
- **Fixes:** a crash caused by an old root-owned install log; `flashkit check`.

## g9 - GitHub install
- `git clone` + `install.sh` sets up everything; Termux:GUI / Termux:API are chosen to match your Termux's signature.
- `flashkit update`, `flashkit plugins`, `flashkit doctor`, `tools/publish.sh`.
- **App:** one persistent root shell instead of a new `su` every few seconds, calmer device checks (and an off switch), a lighter file manager, **Quit completely**.

## App history
- **g8** - Storage tab: look inside zips/tars, extract all or selected, edit zips in place, compress, copy/move/rename/delete.
- **g7** - Cancel stops the whole job; live install log while sideloading.
- **g6 and earlier** - icon UI, GSI in 3 stages, USB mode detection (BROM / EDL / fastbootd), adb/fastboot repair, recovery black-screen fix, job output screen, file browser and console.

```

`README.md`:

```md
<div align="center">

<img src="assets/banner.svg" alt="FlashKit - flash a second Android device over OTG" width="100%">

[![Version](https://img.shields.io/badge/version-g9-9B6BFF?style=for-the-badge&labelColor=0E1626)](CHANGELOG.md)
[![Stars](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/chakradhartode/flashkit?style=for-the-badge&color=FFC857&labelColor=0E1626&logo=github&logoColor=white)](https://github.com/chakradhartode/flashkit/stargazers)
[![Checks](https://img.shields.io/github/actions/workflow/status/chakradhartode/flashkit/check.yml?branch=main&style=for-the-badge&label=checks&labelColor=0E1626)](https://github.com/chakradhartode/flashkit/actions)
[![License](https://img.shields.io/github/license/chakradhartode/flashkit?style=for-the-badge&color=37E08B&labelColor=0E1626)](LICENSE)

[![Termux](https://img.shields.io/badge/Termux-F--Droid%20%7C%20GitHub-2DE2C4?style=for-the-badge&logo=termux&logoColor=white&labelColor=0E1626)](https://github.com/termux/termux-app#installation)
[![Android](https://img.shields.io/badge/Android-root%20needed-4F9BFF?style=for-the-badge&logo=android&logoColor=white&labelColor=0E1626)](#-before-you-start)

**Flash, sideload and rescue a second Android device - straight from your rooted phone.**<br>
No PC. No cables to a computer. Just an OTG cable and Termux.

[**📲 Install**](#-install-in-3-steps) &nbsp;·&nbsp; [**🚀 Use it**](#-open-flashkit) &nbsp;·&nbsp; [**🧭 Features**](#-what-can-it-do) &nbsp;·&nbsp; [**🔄 Update**](#-update) &nbsp;·&nbsp; [**🆘 Help**](#-help--troubleshooting)

</div>

<br>

## ✨ What is FlashKit?

FlashKit turns your rooted phone into a **pocket flashing station**. Plug a second device into it with an OTG cable and
use big, friendly buttons (a real Android app screen, not a wall of text) to sideload ROMs, flash images, do GSI
installs, fix a black-screen recovery, and more. It even has a built-in **file manager with zip tools**.

<table>
<tr>
<td width="62%" valign="top">

- 🧩 **Installs itself** - one command sets up everything, including the Termux:GUI and Termux:API apps.
- 🔐 **Signature-aware** - picks the plugin builds that match *your* Termux, so they actually work together.
- 🛡️ **Careful by design** - confirmations, type-to-confirm for risky partitions, a real **Cancel** button.
- 📜 **Live logs** - see what adb/fastboot (and your recovery) are doing, copy them with one tap.
- 🔄 **One-command updates** - `flashkit update`.

<sub>Home screen shown as an illustration built from the app's real icons.</sub>

</td>
<td width="38%" align="center">
<img src="assets/preview-home.svg" alt="FlashKit home screen" width="260">
</td>
</tr>
</table>

<img src="assets/features.svg" alt="FlashKit features" width="100%">

<br>

## ✅ Before you start

| You need | Why |
|---|---|
| 📱 **A rooted Android phone** (Magisk or KernelSU) | Root is used only for the adb / fastboot calls |
| 🖥️ **Termux** from **[F-Droid](https://f-droid.org/packages/com.termux/)** or **[GitHub](https://github.com/termux/termux-app/releases)** | *Not* the Google Play version - it is outdated and can't run the plugins |
| 🔌 **An OTG cable / adapter** | To connect the device you want to flash |
| 🌐 **Internet** (first install only) | To download packages and the two plugin apps |

> [!TIP]
> Install Termux and its plugins from the **same place** (all F-Droid *or* all GitHub). FlashKit's installer checks this for you.

<br>

## 📲 Install in 3 steps

<img src="assets/how-it-works.svg" alt="Install, auto-setup, open, flash" width="100%">

### Step 1 &nbsp;·&nbsp; Open Termux and paste this one line

```sh
pkg install -y git && git clone https://github.com/chakradhartode/flashkit && bash flashkit/install.sh
```

### Step 2 &nbsp;·&nbsp; Say **Yes** to the pop-ups

The installer does the rest. Android may ask you a few things - this is what to tap:

| You see | Tap |
|---|---|
| *"Allow Termux to access photos, media and files?"* | **Allow** |
| *Superuser request* (Magisk / KernelSU) | **Grant** (tick *remember*) |
| *Package installer* "Do you want to install this app?" (only if you have **no root**) | **Install** - if it says "not allowed", tap **Settings** → turn on **Allow from this source** → go back → **Install** |

> [!NOTE]
> Installing a plugin can make Android **restart Termux** once. That's normal - just open Termux again and the FlashKit menu opens by itself.

### Step 3 &nbsp;·&nbsp; Choose **Start FlashKit**

When the installer finishes, the FlashKit menu opens. Press **Start FlashKit** - the app window appears. 🎉

<details>
<summary><b>What does the installer actually do?</b> (click to see)</summary>

<br>

1. Installs `python`, `android-tools` (adb + fastboot), `git`, `curl`, `termux-api`, `dialog`.
2. Installs the `termuxgui` Python library.
3. Asks for storage access (so it can see your ROM zips).
4. Creates the commands **`flashkit`** and **`start flashkit`** (plus a *FlashKit* entry for the Termux:Widget home-screen widget).
5. Reads your **Termux signing key**, then downloads **Termux:GUI** and **Termux:API** from GitHub / F-Droid - only a build signed with the *same* key is accepted - and installs them (silently with root, or via Android's installer).
6. Opens FlashKit.

Plugins you already have installed and working are never touched. Run `flashkit setup` any time to repeat it safely.

</details>

<br>

## 🚀 Open FlashKit

Type this in Termux:

```sh
flashkit
```

| Menu entry | What it does |
|---|---|
| ▶️ **Start FlashKit** | Opens the app |
| 🔧 **Run manually (text menu)** | The older keyboard-driven version, for when you prefer text |
| ⬇️ **Update FlashKit** | Gets the latest version from GitHub |
| 🧩 **Check / install Termux:GUI + Termux:API** | Re-checks the two plugin apps |
| 🩺 **Doctor** | Checks root, adb/fastboot, plugins, storage permission |
| 🔁 **Open FlashKit when Termux starts** | Turn on to skip typing the command |

Shortcuts: `start flashkit` or `flashkit start` open the app straight away.

<br>

## 🧭 What can it do?

### 🏠 Home - the big buttons

| Button | What it does |
|---|---|
| **Sideload a ROM zip** | Pick a zip → FlashKit checks the device is ready → sideloads it, shows progress and the recovery's install log (live when your recovery allows it) |
| **Flash an image** | Choose a partition (boot, recovery, vbmeta, dtbo...) → pick the `.img` → confirm. Risky partitions need you to type the name |
| **Install an APK** | Install an app on the connected device (adb) |
| **Reboot target** | Reboot to system, recovery, bootloader or fastbootd |
| **Erase a partition** | Three gates (two warnings, then type the exact name) before anything is erased |
| **GSI flash** | Clean or dirty GSI install done in **3 safe stages** (vbmeta → fastbootd → system/super) |
| **Recovery fix** | Fixes the black screen in some recoveries (mounts the vendor partition) |
| **Refresh status / Fix connection** | Re-detect the device, or repair adb/fastboot if they crash |

### 📂 Files · 🗜️ Storage · 💻 Console · ⚙️ Settings

- **Files** - browse the *target* device's storage; push, pull, move and delete.
- **Storage** - your *own phone's* files, with **zip tools**:
  look **inside** a zip/tar without extracting · **extract all** or **only what you pick** · view a file inside · add, delete, rename and move things **inside** a zip · copy, move, rename, delete, compress.
- **Console** - ready-made adb / fastboot commands, or type your own.
- **Settings** - auto-fix, keep-screen-on, live install log, device auto-check, diagnostics, and **Quit completely** (force-stops everything).

📖 Step-by-step walkthroughs: **[docs/GUIDE.md](docs/GUIDE.md)** &nbsp;·&nbsp; 📝 What changed: **[CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md)**

<br>

## 🔄 Update

```sh
flashkit update
```

Gets the newest version from GitHub, shows what's new, and refreshes anything needed. Your settings are kept.

<br>

## 🧰 Command cheat-sheet

| Command | Does |
|---|---|
| `flashkit` | Open the menu |
| `flashkit start` · `start flashkit` | Open the app right away |
| `flashkit manual` | Text-menu version |
| `flashkit update` | Update |
| `flashkit plugins` | Check / install Termux:GUI + Termux:API |
| `flashkit doctor` (or `check`) | Health check |
| `flashkit autostart on` / `off` | Open the menu when Termux starts |
| `flashkit setup` | Re-run the installer |
| `flashkit uninstall` (`-y` for everything, no questions) | Remove FlashKit - asks what to delete (settings, the app files, Termux:GUI/API), or does all of it with `-y` |

<br>

## 🆘 Help & troubleshooting

<details>
<summary><b>"Signature" or "plugin" message / Termux:GUI won't install</b></summary>

<br>

Termux and its plugins only work together if they're signed by the same key. FlashKit reads your Termux's key and picks the matching download automatically. If it says nothing matches, reinstall **Termux, Termux:GUI and Termux:API all from F-Droid** (or all from GitHub), then run `flashkit plugins`.
</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Termux closed while installing</b></summary>

<br>
Normal - Android restarts Termux when a plugin is installed. Open Termux again; the FlashKit menu opens on its own (or type <code>flashkit</code>).
</details>

<details>
<summary><b>"Root denied" / the app can't flash</b></summary>

<br>
Open your root manager (Magisk / KernelSU) and allow **Termux**. Then run <code>flashkit doctor</code> - it should say <i>root works</i>.
</details>

<details>
<summary><b>The target device isn't detected</b></summary>

<br>

- Use a good OTG cable and plug it into the **target** end correctly; try another cable.
- Put the target in the right mode (adb on, **or** bootloader / fastbootd, **or** recovery with sideload).
- Tap **Refresh status** on Home. If the card says adb/fastboot are broken, tap **Fix connection now**.
- MediaTek **BROM** and Qualcomm **EDL** modes are *recognised* and explained, but need other tools (SP Flash Tool, mtkclient, EDL tools).
</details>

<details>
<summary><b>The app window doesn't open</b></summary>

<br>
Run <code>flashkit doctor</code>. Make sure Termux:GUI is installed, then try <code>flashkit plugins</code>. Look at <code>~/flashkit_gui.log</code> for details. If a window still won't appear, allow <b>Display over other apps</b> for Termux and Termux:GUI in Android settings.
</details>

<details>
<summary><b>A job seems stuck</b></summary>

<br>
Tap <b>Cancel job</b> on the job screen - it stops the whole process tree. Then <b>Copy</b> the output if you want to share it.
</details>

<details>
<summary><b>How do I uninstall FlashKit completely?</b></summary>

<br>

```sh
flashkit uninstall -y
```

That removes the `flashkit` / `start flashkit` commands, the Termux:Widget shortcut, the auto-start hook, FlashKit's settings and logs, the app files themselves, and uninstalls Termux:GUI and Termux:API. Run `flashkit uninstall` with no flag to be asked about each of those instead (handy if you want to keep the plugin apps for something else).
</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Where are the logs?</b></summary>

<br>
<code>~/flashkit_gui.log</code> (app) and <code>~/.flashkit/</code> (installer). Open an <a href="https://github.com/chakradhartode/flashkit/issues/new/choose">issue</a> and paste <code>flashkit doctor</code>.
</details>

<br>

## 🔒 Safety

> [!WARNING]
> Flashing can **erase data or make a device unbootable**. Use files made for your exact device, charge both devices, back up what matters, and read each confirmation before you tap. FlashKit is provided as-is, **at your own risk**.

<br>

<div align="center">

<img src="assets/author-card-chakradhar.svg" alt="Made by Chakradhar Tode" width="420" height="105">

If FlashKit saved you a trip to a PC, drop a ⭐ on the repo - it really helps!

<sub>MIT licensed · see <a href="LICENSE">LICENSE</a></sub>

</div>

`bin/flashkit`:

```
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
# FlashKit launcher (installed as $PREFIX/bin/flashkit by install.sh)
SELF="$(readlink -f "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")"
DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$SELF")/.." && pwd)"
PY="${PYTHON:-python}"
VERSION="$(cat "$DIR/VERSION" 2>/dev/null || echo '?')"
CONF="$HOME/.flashkit/config"
LEGACY="${FLASHKIT_LEGACY:-$DIR/legacy/flashkit.sh}"
say() { printf '%s\n' "$*"; }
err() { printf '\033[1;31m%s\033[0m\n' "$*" >&2; }
is_tty() { { [ -t 0 ] && [ -t 1 ]; } || [ -n "${FLASHKIT_FORCE_TTY:-}" ]; }
ask() { [ "${FLASHKIT_YES:-0}" = 1 ] && return 0; printf '%s [y/N] ' "$1"; read -r a; case "$a" in y|Y) return 0 ;; *) return 1 ;; esac; }
have_root_q() { command -v su >/dev/null 2>&1 && timeout 10 su -c id 2>/dev/null | grep -q 'uid=0'; }
android_tool() { env -u LD_PRELOAD -u LD_LIBRARY_PATH PATH="/system/bin:/system/xbin:/vendor/bin:$PATH" "$@"; }
uninstall_pkg() {   # best effort: root removes it silently, otherwise open Android's own uninstall screen
  if have_root_q; then
    su -c 'unset LD_LIBRARY_PATH LD_PRELOAD; export PATH=/system/bin:/system/xbin:/vendor/bin:$PATH; pm uninstall '"$1" >/dev/null 2>&1 \
      && say "  uninstalled $1" || say "  could not uninstall $1 (it may already be gone)"
  else
    android_tool am start -a android.intent.action.DELETE -d package:"$1" >/dev/null 2>&1
    say "  opened the uninstall screen for $1 - confirm there if you want it removed"
  fi
}
conf_get() { sed -n "s/^$1=//p" "$CONF" 2>/dev/null | tail -n1; }
conf_set() { mkdir -p "$(dirname "$CONF")"; touch "$CONF"; { grep -v "^$1=" "$CONF"; echo "$1=$2"; } > "$CONF.tmp"; mv "$CONF.tmp" "$CONF"; }
repo_conf() { sed -n "s/^$1=//p" "$DIR/tools/repo.conf" 2>/dev/null | head -n1; }

usage() { cat <<USAGE
FlashKit $VERSION - flash a second Android device over OTG

  flashkit              open the FlashKit menu (Start / Run manually / Update ...)
  flashkit start        start the app right away          (also:  start flashkit)
  flashkit manual       run the text-menu version by hand
  flashkit update       download the latest version from GitHub
  flashkit plugins      check / install Termux:GUI and Termux:API (signature matched)
  flashkit doctor       check that everything needed is in place (also: flashkit check)
  flashkit autostart on|off    open the menu every time Termux starts
  flashkit setup        (re)install packages, plugins and this command
  flashkit version      show the version
  flashkit uninstall [-y]    remove FlashKit (asks what to delete; -y removes EVERYTHING, no questions)
USAGE
}

run_app() {
  "$PY" -c "import termuxgui" 2>/dev/null || { err "termuxgui is missing - fixing it first"; bash "$DIR/install.sh" --deps-only || return 1; }
  "$PY" "$DIR/flashkit_gui.py" "$@"
  local rc=$?
  if [ $rc -ne 0 ]; then
    err "FlashKit stopped (code $rc). If it says it can't connect to Termux:GUI, run:  flashkit plugins"
    err "Details are in ~/flashkit_gui.log"
  fi
  return $rc
}

run_manual() {
  command -v dialog >/dev/null 2>&1 || pkg install -y dialog || return 1
  bash "$LEGACY"
}

do_update() {
  cd "$DIR" || exit 1
  old="$(cat VERSION 2>/dev/null)"
  if [ -d .git ]; then
    git fetch --quiet origin || { err "Can't reach GitHub - check your internet and try again."; return 1; }
    br="$(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD)"
    if [ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$(git rev-parse "origin/$br")" ]; then
      say "Already up to date ($old)."
    else
      before="$(git rev-parse HEAD)"
      if [ -n "$(git status --porcelain)" ]; then
        git stash push -u -q -m "flashkit-auto-$(date +%s)" && say "Your local edits were saved with:  git -C $DIR stash list"
      fi
      git pull --ff-only --quiet origin "$br" || git reset --hard --quiet "origin/$br"
      say "What's new:"; git log --oneline "$before..HEAD" | head -n 12 | sed 's/^/  /'
    fi
  else
    repo="$(repo_conf GITHUB_REPO)"; branch="$(repo_conf BRANCH)"; branch="${branch:-main}"
    case "$repo" in ""|__GH_REPO__) err "This copy doesn't know its GitHub repo - re-install with git clone."; return 1 ;; esac
    tmp="$(mktemp -d)"
    curl -fsSL "https://codeload.github.com/$repo/tar.gz/refs/heads/$branch" | tar -xz -C "$tmp" --strip-components=1 \
      || { err "Download failed."; rm -rf "$tmp"; return 1; }
    cp -a "$tmp/." "$DIR/" && rm -rf "$tmp"
  fi
  bash "$DIR/install.sh" --update --no-plugins || true
  python "$DIR/tools/termux_plugins.py" --check >/dev/null 2>&1 || python "$DIR/tools/termux_plugins.py" || true
  say ""; say "FlashKit is now $(cat "$DIR/VERSION") (was $old)."
}

do_doctor() {
  say "FlashKit $VERSION in $DIR"
  case "${PREFIX:-}" in *com.termux*) say "  ok   running in Termux" ;; *) say "  FAIL not running in Termux" ;; esac
  command -v python >/dev/null && say "  ok   $(python --version 2>&1)" || say "  FAIL python missing"
  python -c "import termuxgui" 2>/dev/null && say "  ok   termuxgui" || say "  FAIL termuxgui (run: flashkit setup)"
  for t in adb fastboot git curl dialog termux-clipboard-set; do command -v $t >/dev/null && say "  ok   $t" || say "  FAIL $t missing (run: flashkit setup)"; done
  [ -d "$HOME/storage/shared" ] && say "  ok   storage allowed" || say "  FAIL storage not allowed (run: flashkit setup)"
  if command -v su >/dev/null; then timeout 30 su -c id 2>/dev/null | grep -q 'uid=0' && say "  ok   root works" || say "  FAIL root denied/timed out (allow Termux in your root manager)"; else say "  FAIL no su - this phone isn't rooted (needed to flash)"; fi
  python "$DIR/tools/termux_plugins.py" --check
}

add_hook() {
  for rc in "$HOME/.bashrc" "$HOME/.zshrc"; do
    [ "$rc" = "$HOME/.zshrc" ] && [ ! -f "$rc" ] && continue
    grep -qs '# >>> flashkit >>>' "$rc" 2>/dev/null && continue
    cat >> "$rc" <<'HOOK'
# >>> flashkit >>> (added by FlashKit - turn off with:  flashkit autostart off)
if { [ -t 0 ] && [ -t 1 ] || [ -n "${FLASHKIT_FORCE_TTY:-}" ]; } && [ -z "${FLASHKIT_NO_AUTO:-}" ] && command -v flashkit >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  if [ -f "$HOME/.flashkit/open-next" ] || grep -qs '^AUTOSTART=1' "$HOME/.flashkit/config" 2>/dev/null; then
    rm -f "$HOME/.flashkit/open-next"
    flashkit
  fi
fi
# <<< flashkit <<<
HOOK
  done
}
remove_hook() { for rc in "$HOME/.bashrc" "$HOME/.zshrc"; do [ -f "$rc" ] && sed -i '/# >>> flashkit >>>/,/# <<< flashkit <<</d' "$rc"; done; }

do_autostart() {
  case "${1:-status}" in
    on)  add_hook; conf_set AUTOSTART 1; say "FlashKit's menu will open every time you open Termux." ;;
    off) conf_set AUTOSTART 0; say "FlashKit will no longer open by itself." ;;
    *)   [ "$(conf_get AUTOSTART)" = 1 ] && say "autostart is ON" || say "autostart is off" ;;
  esac
}

do_uninstall() {
  for a in "$@"; do case "$a" in -y|--yes) FLASHKIT_YES=1 ;; esac; done
  ask "This removes FlashKit from this phone (you'll be asked what to delete next). Continue?" || { say "Cancelled - nothing was removed."; return 0; }
  rm -f "$PREFIX/bin/flashkit" "$HOME/.shortcuts/FlashKit"; grep -qs 'flashkit start' "$PREFIX/bin/start" 2>/dev/null && rm -f "$PREFIX/bin/start"
  remove_hook
  say "Removed: the flashkit command, 'start flashkit', the Termux:Widget shortcut, and the startup hook."

  if ask "Also delete FlashKit's settings and logs (~/.flashkit_gui.json, ~/flashkit_gui.log, ~/.flashkit)?"; then
    rm -rf "$HOME/.flashkit_gui.json" "$HOME/flashkit_gui.log" "$HOME/.flashkit"; say "Deleted settings and logs."
  fi

  local drop_folder=1
  ask "Delete the FlashKit app files too ($DIR)? This is what makes it a COMPLETE uninstall." || drop_folder=0

  if ask "Also uninstall Termux:GUI and Termux:API from this phone? (skip this if you use them for anything else)"; then
    uninstall_pkg com.termux.gui; uninstall_pkg com.termux.api
  fi

  if [ "$drop_folder" = 1 ]; then
    cd "$HOME" 2>/dev/null || cd /
    rm -rf "$DIR"
    say "Deleted $DIR - FlashKit is fully removed."
  else
    say "Kept $DIR - delete it yourself any time with:  rm -rf $DIR"
  fi
}

pause() { printf '\nPress Enter to go back to the menu... '; read -r _; }

menu_action() {   # returns 99 when the menu should close
  case "$1" in
    1) run_app; return 99 ;;
    2) run_manual; return 99 ;;
    3) do_update; pause ;;
    4) python "$DIR/tools/termux_plugins.py"; pause ;;
    5) do_doctor; pause ;;
    6) if [ "$(conf_get AUTOSTART)" = 1 ]; then do_autostart off; else do_autostart on; fi; pause ;;
    7) say "FlashKit $VERSION"; say "Made by Chakradhar Tode"; say "Repo: https://github.com/$(repo_conf GITHUB_REPO)"; pause ;;
    8) do_uninstall; pause; [ -x "$SELF" ] || return 99 ;;
  esac
  return 0
}

have_dialog() {
  [ "${FLASHKIT_MENU:-}" = text ] && return 1
  command -v dialog >/dev/null 2>&1 || pkg install -y dialog >/dev/null 2>&1
  command -v dialog >/dev/null 2>&1
}

do_menu() {
  local auto c
  while true; do
    auto=off; [ "$(conf_get AUTOSTART)" = 1 ] && auto=ON
    if have_dialog; then
      c="$(dialog --clear --backtitle "Made by Chakradhar Tode" --title " FlashKit $VERSION " --cancel-label "Exit" \
        --menu "Flash a second Android device over OTG" 19 64 8 \
        1 "Start FlashKit" \
        2 "Run manually (text menu)" \
        3 "Update FlashKit" \
        4 "Check / install Termux:GUI + Termux:API" \
        5 "Doctor (check everything)" \
        6 "Open FlashKit when Termux starts: $auto" \
        7 "About" \
        8 "Uninstall FlashKit" 3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)" || { clear; return 0; }
      clear
    else
      printf '\n  FlashKit %s\n  ----------------\n  1) Start FlashKit\n  2) Run manually (text menu)\n  3) Update FlashKit\n  4) Check / install Termux:GUI + Termux:API\n  5) Doctor (check everything)\n  6) Open FlashKit when Termux starts: %s\n  7) About\n  8) Uninstall FlashKit\n  0) Exit\n\nChoose: ' "$VERSION" "$auto"
      read -r c || return 0
      [ "$c" = 0 ] || [ -z "$c" ] && return 0
    fi
    menu_action "$c"; [ $? -eq 99 ] && return 0
  done
}

main() {
  case "${1:-menu}" in
    menu|"") if is_tty; then do_menu; else run_app; fi ;;
    start|run|gui|open) shift; run_app "$@" ;;
    manual|cli|text) run_manual ;;
    update|upgrade) do_update ;;
    setup|install) shift; exec bash "$DIR/install.sh" "$@" ;;
    plugins) shift; exec python "$DIR/tools/termux_plugins.py" "$@" ;;
    doctor|check|status) do_doctor ;;
    autostart) do_autostart "${2:-status}" ;;
    hook) add_hook ;;
    version|-v|--version) say "$VERSION" ;;
    uninstall) shift; do_uninstall "$@" ;;
    help|-h|--help) usage ;;
    *) err "Unknown command: $1"; usage; exit 2 ;;
  esac
}
# one line, so bash has read it ALL before `flashkit update` replaces this file
main "$@"; exit $?

```

`docs/GUIDE.md`:

```md
# 📖 FlashKit guide

Plain steps for everything FlashKit does. New here? Install first: see the [README](../README.md#-install-in-3-steps).

**Contents:** [Home & status](#1-the-home-screen) · [Sideload](#2-sideload-a-rom-zip) · [Flash an image](#3-flash-an-image) · [GSI](#4-gsi-flash-in-3-stages) · [Recovery fix](#5-recovery-black-screen-fix) · [Erase](#6-erase-a-partition) · [Storage & zips](#7-storage--zip-tools-your-phone) · [Console](#8-console) · [Settings & quitting](#9-settings--quitting)

---

## 1. The Home screen

Plug the second device into your phone with the OTG cable. The card at the top tells you what FlashKit sees:

| Status | Meaning | What you can do |
|---|---|---|
| **adb device** | Android is running, USB debugging is on | Install APK, reboot, Files tab, Console |
| **Recovery / sideload** | The device is in recovery (sideload mode) | Sideload a ROM zip |
| **fastboot (bootloader)** | Bootloader mode | Flash an image, erase, GSI stage 1 |
| **fastbootd (userspace)** | Dynamic-partition fastboot | GSI stage 3, flash `system` / `super` |
| **MediaTek BROM / Preloader, Qualcomm EDL, download modes** | Recognised and explained | These need other tools (SP Flash Tool, mtkclient, EDL tools) - FlashKit tells you which |

Tap **Refresh status** to re-check. If adb/fastboot themselves are crashing, a **Fix connection now** button appears.

> **Tip:** FlashKit checks the device every few seconds using root. You can switch that off in **Settings → Check the device automatically**.

## 2. Sideload a ROM zip

1. Put the target in **recovery → "Apply update from ADB"** (sideload). 
2. Home → **Sideload a ROM zip**.
3. Pick the `.zip` (you can also start from the **Storage** tab: tap the zip → *Sideload this zip to the target*).
4. Read the confirmation. The **install log** switch shows what the recovery says while it installs (live if your recovery allows it, otherwise right after).
5. Watch the output. FlashKit ends with a verdict - *succeeded*, *error* or *can't tell* - based on the recovery's log.

The output screen has **Pause scroll / Jump to end**, **Copy**, **Clear** and **Cancel job**.

## 3. Flash an image

1. Put the target in **bootloader** (fastboot) or **fastbootd**.
2. Home → **Flash an image**.
3. Choose the partition: `boot, recovery, vbmeta, vbmeta_system, dtbo, vendor_boot, init_boot, system, vendor, product, super, userdata`.
4. Pick the `.img` file.
5. Confirm. For `vbmeta` there is a switch to **disable verity/verification**. Risky partitions (bootloader, modem, persist, efs...) make you **type the partition name** first.

## 4. GSI flash in 3 stages

Home → **GSI flash**. Pick one mode:

| Mode | What it does | Keeps your data? |
|---|---|---|
| **Clean flash** | vbmeta → fastbootd → delete `product` → flash `system.img` | No |
| **Dirty flash: system.img** | vbmeta → fastbootd → flash `system.img` | Yes |
| **Dirty flash: super.img** | vbmeta → fastbootd → flash `super.img` | Yes |

Then pick **vbmeta.img** (step 1/2) and the **system.img** or **super.img** (step 2/2). After that it runs by itself:

1. **Stage 1** - flashes vbmeta with verification disabled (in the bootloader).
2. **Stage 2** - reboots the target to **fastbootd** and waits until it is ready.
3. **Stage 3** - flashes the system (clean mode frees space first if needed).

A stage only starts if the previous one succeeded, and every stage has **Cancel**.

## 5. Recovery black-screen fix

Some recoveries show a black screen until the vendor partition is mounted. Put the target in recovery → Home → **Recovery fix**. FlashKit waits for the device and runs the mount for you. (The same command is in **Console → adb**.)

## 6. Erase a partition

Home → **Erase a partition** → three gates: two warnings, then **type the exact partition name**. There is no undo.

## 7. Storage & zip tools (your phone)

The **Storage** tab is a file manager for *your* phone (Internal, Termux and SD card).

**Everyday tasks**
- Tap **Select**, tick items, then **Copy / Move / Rename / Delete / Compress**.
- After Copy or Move, open the destination folder and tap **Paste here**.
- **New folder** makes a folder; the search box filters the list.

**Look inside a zip (without extracting)**
1. Tap a `.zip` (also `.apk`, `.tar`, `.tar.gz`, `.tar.xz`, `.tar.bz2`, `.gz`, `.xz`, `.bz2`).
2. Browse its folders. Tap a file → **View (without extracting)** to read it.

**Extract**
- **Extract all** - everything, into a folder named after the zip (or wherever you choose).
- **Only what you need** - tap **Select**, tick the files/folders, tap **Extract**.
- Choose *where to*, what to do **if a name already exists** (keep both / replace / skip), keep or flatten the folder structure, and give a **password** if the zip asks.
- Unsafe paths (`../`) are blocked, free space is checked first, and a bad file never stops the rest.

**Edit a zip in place** (zip files only)
- Inside a zip: **Select** → **Delete / Rename / Move / Copy**, or **Add files** from your phone. 
- Edits don't recompress your data, so even a big ROM zip is quick, and the original is left untouched if anything fails.

**Make a zip** - Select files/folders → **Compress** → pick a name and level (Store / Fast / Normal / Maximum).

## 8. Console

**Console** has ready-made adb and fastboot commands (device info, logs, reboots, *Mount vendor*, *Recovery's log*...) and a box for your own command. Each result opens on its own screen with **Copy**.

## 9. Settings & quitting

| Setting | What it does |
|---|---|
| Auto-fix connection problems | Restarts adb or re-detects when the link drops |
| Keep the screen on while a job runs | Stops the phone sleeping mid-flash |
| Show the recovery's install log | Live log while sideloading |
| Check the device automatically | Turn off to check only when you tap Refresh |
| **Quit completely (force stop)** | Stops jobs, stops adb and force-stops Termux:GUI so nothing runs in the background |

Need more help? See [Help & troubleshooting](../README.md#-help--troubleshooting) or open an [issue](https://github.com/chakradhartode/flashkit/issues).

```

`flashkit_gui.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""FlashKit GUI - native Android UI (Termux:GUI) for flashing a SECOND device
with adb/fastboot over OTG from a rooted phone.

Run as your normal Termux user (NOT through su):

    python flashkit_gui.py

Root is only used for the actual adb/fastboot calls (su -c ...). Anything that
goes wrong is shown on screen and written to ~/flashkit_gui.log.
"""
import os
import re
import sys
import base64
import json
import time
import queue
import shlex
import shutil
import itertools
import select
import zipfile
import tarfile
import gzip
import bz2
import lzma
import zlib
import struct
import errno
import codecs
import threading
import subprocess
import traceback

VERSION = "g9"
HOME = os.path.expanduser("~")
BASE = "/data/data/com.termux/files"
# su's shell starts with a bare environment: without these, adb can't find
# its binaries by name and crashes trying to mkdir '//.android'.
ENV = ('export HOME="%s/home" TMPDIR="%s/usr/tmp" '
       'PATH="%s/usr/bin:$PATH:/system/bin:/system/xbin:/sbin"; ' % (BASE, BASE, BASE))
STORAGE_ROOT = os.environ.get("FLASHKIT_ROOT") or (
    "/storage/emulated/0" if os.path.isdir("/storage/emulated/0") else "/sdcard")
DOWNLOAD = os.path.join(STORAGE_ROOT, "Download")
REMOTE_ROOT = os.environ.get("FLASHKIT_REMOTE_ROOT") or "/sdcard"
LOGFILE = os.path.join(HOME, "flashkit_gui.log")
CFGFILE = os.path.join(HOME, ".flashkit_gui.json")
PROBE_EVERY = float(os.environ.get("FLASHKIT_PROBE_EVERY", "3"))
tg = None  # the termuxgui module, imported in main() after bootstrap


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ basics
def rgb(v):
    """0xRRGGBB -> signed 32-bit ARGB int (what the plugin expects)."""
    v = (v & 0xFFFFFF) | 0xFF000000
    return v - (1 << 32) if v >= (1 << 31) else v


BG = rgb(0x101318)
SURF = rgb(0x1B1F27)
SURF2 = rgb(0x2A303C)
TEXT = rgb(0xE3E2E6)
MUTED = rgb(0x9AA3B2)
ACCENT = rgb(0xAFC6FF)
PRIM_BG = rgb(0x2D4275)
PRIM_TX = rgb(0xDDE6FF)
PILL = rgb(0x3A4560)
GOOD = rgb(0x8EE2A0)
WARN = rgb(0xFFCB6B)
BAD = rgb(0xFF8A80)
BAD_BG = rgb(0x5F1A17)
BAD_TX = rgb(0xFFDAD6)

KINDS = {  # button kind -> (background, text colour)
    "tonal": (SURF2, TEXT),
    "primary": (PRIM_BG, PRIM_TX),
    "danger": (BAD_BG, BAD_TX),
    "flat": (SURF, ACCENT),
    "nav": (BG, MUTED),
    "navsel": (PILL, PRIM_TX),
}


_ICON_CACHE = {}


def icon_bytes(name):
    """PNG bytes of an embedded icon, or None if it isn't there."""
    if name not in _ICON_CACHE:
        data = _ICON_B64.get(name)
        _ICON_CACHE[name] = base64.b64decode(data) if data else None
    return _ICON_CACHE[name]


class Pill:
    """A pill-shaped button: [round cap][coloured Button][round cap]. Looks like one rounded button
    but answers to the same few calls as a plain Button (text, visibility, width...)."""

    def __init__(self, box, btn):
        self.box, self.btn, self.id = box, btn, btn.id

    def setvisibility(self, v):
        safe(self.box.setvisibility, v)
        safe(self.btn.setvisibility, v)

    def setwidth(self, w):
        safe(self.box.setwidth, w)

    def __getattr__(self, name):  # settext, settextcolor, setgravity, ... go to the Button itself
        return getattr(self.btn, name)


def log(msg):
    try:
        if os.path.exists(LOGFILE) and os.path.getsize(LOGFILE) > 300000:
            os.replace(LOGFILE, LOGFILE + ".old")
        with open(LOGFILE, "a") as f:
            f.write("%s %s\n" % (time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"), msg))
    except Exception:
        pass


def safe(fn, *a, **k):
    """Cosmetic calls must never take the app down."""
    try:
        return fn(*a, **k)
    except Exception as e:
        log("safe(%s) failed: %r" % (getattr(fn, "__name__", fn), e))
        return None


def shq(s):
    return shlex.quote(str(s))


def router(fn):
    """Mark an entry function that only decides WHICH screen to show and then
    forwards to it with go(). Routers must never sit on the back stack: Back
    would re-run them and they'd bounce straight forward again, so the screen
    just redraws and you can never leave."""
    fn._router = True
    return fn


def human(n):
    if n is None:
        return ""
    n = float(n)
    for u in ("B", "KB", "MB", "GB"):
        if n < 1024 or u == "GB":
            return ("%d B" % n) if u == "B" else ("%.1f %s" % (n, u))
        n /= 1024.0


def load_cfg():
    cfg = {"autofix": True, "keepon": True, "sideload_log": True, "autodetect": True}
    try:
        with open(CFGFILE) as f:
            cfg.update(json.load(f))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return cfg


def save_cfg(cfg):
    try:
        with open(CFGFILE, "w") as f:
            json.dump(cfg, f)
    except Exception:
        pass


# ------------------------------------------------------------- running things
class RootShell:
    """ONE long-lived root shell for the quick commands (device checks, listings...), so Superuser is asked once
    instead of on every check. Each command runs in a subshell with stdin closed, so an `exit` or a command that
    reads input can't break it. A command that hangs is killed and the shell restarted."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.p = None

    def close(self):
        p, self.p = self.p, None
        if p is not None:
            for fn in (p.stdin.close, p.kill):
                try:
                    fn()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    def _start(self):
        self.p = subprocess.Popen(["su"], stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, bufsize=0)
        self.p.stdin.write((ENV + "\n").encode())

    def run(self, cmd, timeout=30):
        with self.lock:
            tag = "@@FK" + os.urandom(4).hex() + "@@"
            pat = re.compile(re.escape(("\n" + tag).encode()) + rb"(\d+)\n")
            script = "( " + cmd + "\n) </dev/null 2>&1\nFK_RC=$?\nprintf '\\n" + tag + "%d\\n' \"$FK_RC\"\n"
            for attempt in (0, 1):
                try:
                    if self.p is None or self.p.poll() is not None:
                        self._start()
                    self.p.stdin.write(script.encode())
                    self.p.stdin.flush()
                    fd, buf, end = self.p.stdout.fileno(), b"", time.time() + timeout
                    while True:
                        m = pat.search(buf)
                        if m:
                            return int(m.group(1)), buf[:m.start()].decode("utf-8", "replace").strip()
                        left = end - time.time()
                        if left <= 0:
                            self.close()
                            return 124, "(timed out after %ss)" % timeout
                        if select.select([fd], [], [], min(left, 1.0))[0]:
                            chunk = os.read(fd, 65536)
                            if not chunk:
                                raise EOFError()
                            buf += chunk
                except FileNotFoundError:
                    self.p = None
                    return 127, "su not found - is this phone rooted?"
                except (EOFError, OSError):
                    self.close()
                    if attempt == 1:
                        return 1, "the root shell stopped answering"
            return 1, "the root shell stopped answering"


ROOT = RootShell()


def su_run(cmd, timeout=30):
    """Blocking root command -> (returncode, merged output), through the one persistent root shell."""
    return ROOT.run(cmd, timeout)


def run_user(cmd, timeout=25):
    """Blocking command as the normal Termux user (no su) -> (rc, merged output)."""
    try:
        p = subprocess.run(["sh", "-c", cmd], stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                           stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=timeout)
        return p.returncode, p.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip()
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return 124, "(timed out after %ss)" % timeout
    except Exception as e:
        return 1, "error: %s" % e


# What it looks like when adb/fastboot themselves can't even start (as opposed to
# starting fine and finding no device): a linker/library error, a crash, a missing file.
TOOL_ERR = re.compile(r"CANNOT LINK EXECUTABLE|cannot locate symbol|library \".+\" not found|"
                      r"inaccessible or not found|: not found|Segmentation fault|Exec format error|"
                      r"cannot execute|unexpected e_type|bad ELF", re.I)


def tool_error(text):
    """First line of `text` that says a program failed to start, else ''."""
    for line in (text or "").splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if line and TOOL_ERR.search(line):
            return line[:200]
    return ""


def tools_broken(st):
    te = st.get("tool_err") or {}
    return bool(te.get("adb") or te.get("fastboot"))


_JOB_SEQ = itertools.count(1)

# Cancel = stop the WHOLE job: its shell script, loops, sleeps and every adb/fastboot it started.
# Every job runs with FLASHKIT_JOB=<token> in its environment (children inherit it), so we find
# them through /proc and kill them all. pkill on the command-line marker and the old blanket
# adb/fastboot kill are only fallbacks for systems where that scan finds nothing.
_KILL = r"""
TOK=@@TOK@@
FOUND=0
kill_job() {
  for f in $(grep -la "FLASHKIT_JOB=$TOK" /proc/[0-9]*/environ 2>/dev/null); do
    p=${f#/proc/}; p=${p%/environ}
    [ "$p" = "$$" ] && continue
    kill -9 "$p" 2>/dev/null && FOUND=$((FOUND+1))
  done
}
kill_job; sleep 0.3; kill_job
pkill -9 -f "FLASHKIT_JO[B]_$TOK" 2>/dev/null
sleep 0.2; kill_job
if [ "$FOUND" -eq 0 ]; then pkill -9 -x fastboot 2>/dev/null; pkill -9 -x adb 2>/dev/null; fi
exit 0
"""


def kill_script(token):
    return _KILL.replace("@@TOK@@", token)


class Job:
    """A command streamed line by line from a worker thread - as root (su) by
    default, or as the normal Termux user with root=False (package repair).
    cancel() really stops it (see _KILL)."""

    def __init__(self, title, script, timeout=600, post=None, root=True):
        self.title, self.script, self.timeout, self.post, self.root = title, script, timeout, post, root
        self.q = queue.Queue()
        self.rc = None
        self.cancelled = False
        self.cancel_started = False
        self.ended = False
        self.proc = None
        self.token = "fk%x_%d" % (os.getpid(), next(_JOB_SEQ))

    def start(self):
        threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True).start()

    def _finish(self):
        if not self.ended:
            self.ended = True
            self.q.put(None)

    def cancel(self):
        """Stop the job for real. -> False if it is already being stopped."""
        if self.cancel_started:
            return False
        self.cancel_started = self.cancelled = True
        threading.Thread(target=self._kill, daemon=True).start()
        return True

    def _kill(self):
        for _ in range(25):  # it may not have launched its process yet
            if self.proc is not None:
                break
            time.sleep(0.2)
        try:
            (su_run if self.root else run_user)(kill_script(self.token), 15)
        except Exception:
            log("kill script failed: " + traceback.format_exc())
        p = self.proc
        for _ in range(25):  # let it wind down, then end our side of the pipe
            if p is None or p.poll() is not None:
                return
            time.sleep(0.2)
        try:
            p.kill()
        except Exception:
            pass
        time.sleep(2.0)
        if not self.ended:  # even that didn't unblock the reader: free the screen anyway
            if self.rc is None:
                self.rc = -9
            self._finish()

    def _run(self):
        marker = "FLASHKIT_JOB_" + self.token
        wrapped = ("export FLASHKIT_JOB=%s; if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then timeout %d sh -c %s %s; "
                   "else sh -c %s %s; fi" % (self.token, self.timeout, shq(self.script), marker,
                                             shq(self.script), marker))
        try:
            if self.root:
                p = subprocess.Popen(["su", "-c", ENV + wrapped], stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                                     stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
            else:
                p = subprocess.Popen(["sh", "-c", wrapped], stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                                     stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                     env=dict(os.environ, DEBIAN_FRONTEND="noninteractive"))
            self.proc = p
            dec = codecs.getincrementaldecoder("utf-8")("replace")
            fd = p.stdout.fileno()
            while True:
                chunk = os.read(fd, 4096)
                if not chunk:
                    break
                self.q.put(dec.decode(chunk))
            p.wait()
            if self.rc is None:
                self.rc = p.returncode
        except Exception as e:
            self.q.put("\n[could not run: %s]\n" % e)
            self.rc = 1
        if self.post and not self.cancelled:
            try:
                self.q.put("\n" + self.post())
            except Exception as e:
                self.q.put("\n[analysis failed: %s]\n" % e)
        self._finish()


class LogBuf:
    """Turns raw tool output into lines; \\r rewrites the current line so progress bars don't flood
    the screen. Keeps up to `keep` lines (that is what Copy copies) but only the last `tail` are
    shown, so a long job never makes the screen slow."""

    def __init__(self, keep=6000, tail=350):
        self.lines, self.cur, self.cr = [], "", False
        self.keep, self.tail, self.dropped = keep, tail, 0

    def feed(self, s):
        if "\r" not in s and not self.cr:  # fast path: plain text
            parts = s.split("\n")
            self.cur += parts[0]
            for part in parts[1:]:
                self.lines.append(self.cur)
                self.cur = part
        else:
            for ch in s:
                if ch == "\r":
                    self.cr = True
                elif ch == "\n":
                    self.lines.append(self.cur)
                    self.cur, self.cr = "", False
                else:
                    if self.cr:
                        self.cur, self.cr = "", False
                    self.cur += ch
        if len(self.lines) > self.keep:
            extra = len(self.lines) - self.keep
            del self.lines[:extra]
            self.dropped += extra

    def _all(self):
        return self.lines + ([self.cur] if self.cur else [])

    def text(self):
        lines = self._all()
        if len(lines) > self.tail:
            hidden = len(lines) - self.tail + self.dropped
            lines = ["... %d earlier lines hidden (Copy copies everything) ..." % hidden] + lines[-self.tail:]
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def full_text(self):
        head = ["[%d earlier lines were dropped]" % self.dropped] if self.dropped else []
        return "\n".join(head + self._all())


def copy_to_clipboard(text):
    """-> ('clipboard', None) or ('file', path) or ('fail', error). Uses Termux:API's
    termux-clipboard-set when it works; otherwise saves a text file in Download."""
    exe = shutil.which("termux-clipboard-set")
    if exe:
        try:
            p = subprocess.run([exe], input=text.encode("utf-8", "replace"), timeout=8,
                               stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
            if p.returncode == 0:
                return "clipboard", None
        except Exception:
            pass
    try:
        os.makedirs(DOWNLOAD, exist_ok=True)
        path = os.path.join(DOWNLOAD, "flashkit_output_%s.txt" % time.strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S"))
        with open(path, "w") as f:
            f.write(text)
        return "file", path
    except Exception as e:
        return "fail", str(e)


PCT = re.compile(r"(\d{1,3})%")


# --------------------------------------------------------- device detection
def uinfo_script(root="/sys/bus/usb/devices"):
    """Shell snippet: one DEV| line per real (non-hub) USB device, then IF| lines for its
    interfaces (class/subclass/protocol). Tells us WHAT is plugged in even when adb and
    fastboot can't talk to it (MediaTek BROM/Preloader, MTP-only, black-screen recovery...)."""
    return (
        'for d in %s/*; do [ -f "$d/idVendor" ] || continue; v=$(cat "$d/idVendor" 2>/dev/null); '
        '[ "$v" = 1d6b ] && continue; '
        'echo "DEV|$(basename "$d")|$v:$(cat "$d/idProduct" 2>/dev/null)|$(cat "$d/manufacturer" 2>/dev/null)'
        '|$(cat "$d/product" 2>/dev/null)"; '
        'for i in "$d":*; do [ -f "$i/bInterfaceClass" ] && echo "IF|$(cat "$i/bInterfaceClass")/'
        '$(cat "$i/bInterfaceSubClass")/$(cat "$i/bInterfaceProtocol")"; done; '
        'done' % root)


PROBE_SCRIPT = ('chmod -R 666 /dev/bus/usb/*/* 2>/dev/null; '
                'echo @@USB; ls /dev/bus/usb/*/* 2>/dev/null; '
                'echo @@ADB; adb devices 2>&1; '
                'FBOUT=$(fastboot devices 2>&1); echo @@FB; echo "$FBOUT"; '
                'if printf "%s\n" "$FBOUT" | grep -Eq "^[^[:space:]]+[[:space:]]+[Ff]astboot"; then '
                'echo @@FBU; if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then timeout 8 fastboot getvar is-userspace 2>&1; '
                'else fastboot getvar is-userspace 2>&1; fi; fi; '
                'echo @@UINFO; ' + uinfo_script() + '; echo @@END')
ADB_LINE = re.compile(r"^(\S+)\s+(device|offline|unauthorized|recovery|sideload|bootloader|"
                      r"rescue|authorizing|connecting|detached|host|no permissions)\b")
FB_LINE = re.compile(r"^(\S+)\s+[Ff]astboot\w*")
USB_LINE = re.compile(r"^/dev/bus/usb/(\d+)/(\d+)$")


def probe():
    rc, out = su_run(PROBE_SCRIPT, timeout=20)
    st = {"root": True, "err": "", "usb": 0, "adb": [], "fb": [], "model": "",
          "fixed": "", "tool_err": {}, "fastbootd": None, "usbinfo": [], "t": time.time()}
    if "@@END" not in out:
        st["root"] = False
        st["err"] = out.strip() or "su gave no output (rc=%s)" % rc
        return st
    sec = None
    raw = {"ADB": [], "FB": []}
    for line in out.splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if line.startswith("@@"):
            sec = line[2:]
            continue
        if sec in raw:
            raw[sec].append(line)
        if sec == "USB":
            m = USB_LINE.match(line)
            if m and int(m.group(2)) > 1:  # dev 001 is the root hub
                st["usb"] += 1
        elif sec == "ADB":
            m = ADB_LINE.match(line)
            if m:
                st["adb"].append((m.group(1), m.group(2)))
        elif sec == "FB":
            m = FB_LINE.match(line)
            if m:
                st["fb"].append(m.group(1))
        elif sec == "FBU":
            m = re.search(r"is-userspace:\s*(yes|no)", line, re.I)
            if m:
                st["fastbootd"] = m.group(1).lower() == "yes"
        elif sec == "UINFO":
            if line.startswith("DEV|"):
                parts = line.split("|")
                st["usbinfo"].append({"bus": parts[1] if len(parts) > 1 else "",
                                      "id": parts[2] if len(parts) > 2 else "",
                                      "mfr": parts[3] if len(parts) > 3 else "",
                                      "product": parts[4] if len(parts) > 4 else "", "ifs": []})
            elif line.startswith("IF|") and st["usbinfo"]:
                st["usbinfo"][-1]["ifs"].append(line[3:])
    st["tool_err"] = {"adb": tool_error("\n".join(raw["ADB"])), "fastboot": tool_error("\n".join(raw["FB"]))}
    return st


USB_HINTS = {
    "brom": "Looks like the target is in MediaTek BROM mode, the chip's lowest-level USB mode. adb and "
            "fastboot can't talk to it. Unplug it, power it fully off, then boot it to recovery or the "
            "bootloader with its key combo. Flashing from BROM needs a BROM tool (SP Flash Tool or "
            "mtkclient), not adb/fastboot.",
    "preloader": "Looks like the target is in MediaTek Preloader mode. Every MediaTek device shows this for a "
                 "moment at boot; if it STAYS like this it isn't booting any further. adb and fastboot can't "
                 "talk to it. Power it fully off, then boot it to recovery or the bootloader.",
    "edl": "Qualcomm EDL (emergency download) mode. adb and fastboot can't talk to it.",
    "samsungdl": "Samsung Download mode. adb and fastboot can't talk to it. Use Odin on a PC, or hold the "
                 "keys to reboot it.",
    "adb": "The target exposes an ADB interface but adb isn't listing it. Tap Fix connection now. If the target "
           "shows 'Allow USB debugging?', accept it. A custom recovery with a black screen can take a minute to "
           "bring adb up: wait, then Refresh.",
    "fastboot": "The target exposes a fastboot interface but fastboot isn't listing it. Tap Fix connection now, "
                "or re-plug it.",
    "mtp": "The target is in file-transfer/charging mode, not adb. Turn on USB debugging (booted Android) or "
           "boot it to recovery/bootloader. A recovery that shows nothing may not have started adb yet: wait a "
           "minute, then Refresh.",
    "other": "adb and fastboot can't talk to it. Turn on USB debugging on the target, or boot it to "
             "bootloader/recovery.",
}


def usb_kind(dev):
    """-> (kind, label) for one entry of st['usbinfo']."""
    vid, _, pid = (dev.get("id") or "").partition(":")
    ifs = dev.get("ifs") or []
    name = " ".join(x for x in (dev.get("mfr"), dev.get("product")) if x)
    low = (name + " " + (dev.get("id") or "")).lower()
    if vid == "0e8d":
        if pid == "0003" or "brom" in low:
            return "brom", "MediaTek BROM mode"
        if pid in ("2000", "2001") or "preloader" in low or "vcom" in low:
            return "preloader", "MediaTek Preloader mode"
    if vid == "05c6" and pid == "9008":
        return "edl", "Qualcomm EDL mode"
    if vid == "04e8" and pid == "685d":
        return "samsungdl", "Samsung Download mode"
    if "ff/42/01" in ifs:
        return "adb", "ADB interface"
    if "ff/42/03" in ifs:
        return "fastboot", "fastboot interface"
    if any(i.startswith("06/") for i in ifs) or "mtp" in low:
        return "mtp", "file-transfer (MTP) mode"
    return "other", name or "unknown USB mode"


def usb_headline(st):
    """('USB: <what it is> (vid:pid)', kind) for the first real USB device, or None."""
    devs = st.get("usbinfo") or []
    if not devs:
        return None
    kind, label = usb_kind(devs[0])
    head = "USB: %s (%s)" % (label, devs[0].get("id", "?"))
    if len(devs) > 1:
        head += "  +%d more" % (len(devs) - 1)
    return head, kind


def describe(st):
    """(headline, colour, hint) for a probe result."""
    if not st.get("root", True):
        return ("Root access missing", BAD,
                "Allow superuser for Termux in Magisk/KernelSU, then tap Refresh. " + st.get("err", ""))
    adb, fb = st["adb"], st["fb"]
    if tools_broken(st) and not (adb or fb):
        te = st["tool_err"]
        names = [t for t in ("adb", "fastboot") if te.get(t)]
        which = " and ".join(names)
        what = ("The %s programs in Termux crash the moment they launch" if len(names) > 1
                else "The %s program in Termux crashes the moment it launches") % which
        return ("%s can't start" % which, BAD,
                what + " (a library error), so nothing can be detected. Your cable and the target are not "
                "the problem. Tap Repair adb/fastboot.")
    if fb:
        if st.get("fastbootd") is True:
            return ("Fastbootd  \u00b7  " + fb[0], GOOD,
                    "Userspace fastboot (fastbootd): this is where logical partitions like system, product "
                    "and vendor can be deleted and flashed.")
        return ("Fastboot  \u00b7  " + fb[0], GOOD,
                "Bootloader mode: flash, erase, unlock and fastboot commands are available.")
    if adb:
        ser, s = adb[0]
        if s == "device":
            return ("ADB  \u00b7  " + ser, GOOD,
                    "Booted normally: sideload, install, file transfer and reboot are available.")
        if s == "sideload":
            return ("ADB sideload  \u00b7  " + ser, WARN, "Waiting for a zip - use Sideload.")
        if s == "recovery":
            return ("Recovery  \u00b7  " + ser, WARN,
                    "In recovery: file browsing and reboot work. For a ROM zip pick "
                    "'Apply update > ADB' on the target.")
        if s == "unauthorized":
            return ("Unauthorized  \u00b7  " + ser, WARN,
                    "Accept the 'Allow USB debugging?' prompt on the target's screen.")
        if s == "offline":
            return ("ADB offline  \u00b7  " + ser, WARN, "Reconnecting automatically...")
        return ("ADB %s  \u00b7  %s" % (s, ser), WARN, "")
    if st["usb"] > 0:
        uh = usb_headline(st)
        if uh:
            return (uh[0], WARN, USB_HINTS[uh[1]])
        return ("USB device seen, not answering", WARN,
                "It shows up on USB but neither adb nor fastboot replies. Turn on USB debugging "
                "on the target, or boot it to bootloader/recovery.")
    return ("No device connected", MUTED,
            "Plug the target into the OTG adapter (use a data cable, not charge-only).")


REPAIR_INFO = (
    "adb/fastboot can't start on this phone: Termux's own adb/fastboot programs fail with a library "
    "error. That isn't caused by your cable or the target.\n\n"
    "This will:\n"
    "1. refresh Termux's package lists (needs internet)\n"
    "2. upgrade Termux's packages so the libraries match\n"
    "3. reinstall adb, fastboot and the C++ runtime\n"
    "4. check that they start\n\n"
    "It can take several minutes. Only run it on the phone where adb/fastboot is broken. If it works "
    "afterwards, close FlashKit and open it again.")

REPAIR_SCRIPT = r"""
echo "== 1/4: refreshing package lists (needs internet) =="
if ! pkg update -y; then
  echo "!! Could not reach Termux's package servers. Check the internet connection and run this again."
  exit 1
fi
echo "== 2/4: upgrading Termux packages so libraries and tools match =="
apt-get -y -o Dpkg::Options::=--force-confdef -o Dpkg::Options::=--force-confold full-upgrade \
  || echo "!! the upgrade reported a problem - carrying on"
echo "== 3/4: reinstalling adb, fastboot and the C++ runtime =="
apt-get -y install --reinstall android-tools libc++ \
  || echo "!! the reinstall reported a problem - carrying on"
echo "== 4/4: checking that they start =="
adb version 2>&1 | head -n 3
fastboot --version 2>&1 | head -n 2
exit 0
"""

TOOLS_CHECK = 'adb version 2>&1 | head -n 3; echo @@; fastboot --version 2>&1 | head -n 3'


def tool_health(root=True):
    """Can adb and fastboot start? -> {'adb': error line or '', 'fastboot': ..., 'raw': text}."""
    rc, out = su_run(TOOLS_CHECK, 25) if root else run_user(TOOLS_CHECK)
    a, _, f = out.partition("@@")
    return {"adb": tool_error(a), "fastboot": tool_error(f), "raw": out}


def tools_verdict():
    """After the repair: test as normal user AND as root, and explain what it means."""
    out = ["-------- result --------"]
    u, r = tool_health(root=False), tool_health(root=True)
    ubad, rbad = u["adb"] or u["fastboot"], r["adb"] or r["fastboot"]
    if not ubad and not rbad:
        out.append("OK adb and fastboot start fine now (normal user and root).")
        out.append("Close FlashKit and open it again so it picks up the repaired files.")
        return "\n".join(out)
    err = ubad or rbad
    out.append("X still can't start: " + err)
    if ubad and not rbad:
        out.append("  It fails as the normal Termux user but works as root - an odd mix. "
                   "Send me this screen.")
        return "\n".join(out)
    if rbad and not ubad:
        out.append("  It works as the normal Termux user but fails when run as root, so the problem is "
                   "the root environment, not the packages. Send me this screen.")
        return "\n".join(out)
    m = re.search(r'cannot locate symbol "([^"]+)"', err)
    if m:
        rc, found = run_user("grep -l -a -F -- %s %s/usr/lib/*.so* 2>/dev/null" % (shq(m.group(1)), BASE))
        libs = [os.path.basename(x) for x in found.splitlines() if x.strip()]
        out.append("  Missing symbol: " + m.group(1))
        out.append("  Libraries on this phone that contain it: " + (", ".join(libs[:8]) or "none"))
        if "libc++_shared.so" in libs:
            out.append("  libc++_shared.so has it, so another copy is probably being loaded instead. "
                       "Send me this screen.")
        else:
            out.append("  libc++_shared.so does NOT have it: Termux's C++ library and its adb/fastboot "
                       "build don't match.")
    rc, pol = run_user('apt-cache policy libc++ android-tools 2>/dev/null | grep -E "^[a-z]|Installed|Candidate"')
    if pol:
        out.append("  Package versions:")
        out.extend("    " + x for x in pol.splitlines())
    rc, arch = run_user("uname -m")
    out.append("  CPU: " + (arch or "?"))
    out.append("")
    out.append("This looks like a packaging mismatch inside Termux, not your cable, adapter or the target "
               "(another Termux user reported the same error on a different program on 30 Sep 2026). "
               "What you can do: try again later, or run termux-change-repo in Termux to pick a different "
               "mirror and then Repair again, or use a device where adb/fastboot already works as the host.")
    return "\n".join(out)


_RECOVERY_FIX = r"""
adbx() { @@A@@ "$@"; }
echo "== waiting for the target to show up on adb (up to @@SECS@@ s) =="
i=0; st=""
while [ "$i" -lt @@TRIES@@ ]; do
  st=$(adbx get-state 2>/dev/null)
  case "$st" in recovery|sideload|rescue|device) break ;; esac
  st=""
  [ $((i % 5)) -eq 4 ] && echo "...still waiting"
  sleep @@PAUSE@@; i=$((i+1))
done
if [ -z "$st" ]; then
  echo "!! Nothing showed up on adb. A black-screen recovery can take a minute to start adb."
  echo "   Look at the status line on Home: it says what the USB device actually is."
  echo "   Wait a little, then run this again."
  exit 2
fi
echo "adb sees it: $st"
if [ "$st" = "device" ]; then
  echo "!! The target is booted into Android, not into the custom recovery. Boot it into recovery first."
  exit 3
fi
echo "== mount vendor =="
OUT=$(adbx shell mount -t ext4 /dev/block/mapper/vendor /vendor 2>&1); RC=$?
[ -n "$OUT" ] && echo "$OUT"
if adbx shell mount 2>/dev/null | grep -q " /vendor "; then
  echo "== vendor is mounted =="
  adbx shell "ls /vendor | head -n 8"
  echo "Check the target's screen now. If it is still black, tell me what it does."
  exit 0
fi
echo "!! vendor is still not mounted (mount exit $RC)."
echo "-- what the recovery has in /dev/block/mapper --"
adbx shell "ls -l /dev/block/mapper" 2>&1
exit 1
"""


def recovery_fix_script(adb="adb", tries=45, pause=2):
    """The recovery developer's fix for the black screen (mount vendor), run as soon as the
    target shows up in recovery - no PC needed."""
    vals = {"A": adb, "TRIES": str(tries), "PAUSE": str(pause), "SECS": str(int(tries * pause))}
    return re.sub(r"@@(\w+)@@", lambda m: vals[m.group(1)], _RECOVERY_FIX)


# Sideloading a zip: the host only sees the transfer percentage. The recovery shows its INSTALL
# log (what a custom recovery prints on its own screen) - FlashKit fetches it from /tmp/recovery.log:
#  * live, while the zip installs, IF the recovery lets adb run a shell during sideload (most
#    TWRP/OrangeFox builds don't: they switch adb to a sideload-only mode), else
#  * the moment the recovery is back on adb, with a guess at whether the install worked.
_SIDELOAD = r"""
adbx() { @@A@@ "$@"; }
ZIP=@@ZIP@@
@@REBOOT@@
echo "== sending the zip =="
echo "+ @@A@@ sideload $ZIP"
LAST=-1; NOTED=0; LIVE_SHOWN=0
poll_log() {
  if [ "$LAST" -lt 0 ]; then
    CNT=$(adbx shell "wc -l < /tmp/recovery.log" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r ')
    case "$CNT" in
      ''|*[!0-9]*)
        if [ "$NOTED" = 0 ]; then
          NOTED=1
          echo "(this recovery doesn't allow adb shell while it receives a zip, so its install log can't be shown live - you get it the moment it finishes)"
        fi
        return 1 ;;
    esac
    LAST=$CNT
    echo "(live recovery log connected)"
    return 0
  fi
  NEW=$(adbx shell "tail -n +$((LAST+1)) /tmp/recovery.log" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r')
  [ -n "$NEW" ] || return 0
  printf '%s\n' "$NEW" | while IFS= read -r l; do echo "[recovery] $l"; done
  LAST=$((LAST + $(printf '%s\n' "$NEW" | wc -l)))
  LIVE_SHOWN=1
  return 0
}
adbx sideload "$ZIP" &
SP=$!
while kill -0 "$SP" 2>/dev/null; do
  @@POLL@@
  sleep 2
done
wait "$SP"; RC=$?
echo "== adb sideload finished (exit $RC) =="
echo "== waiting for the recovery to finish and report (up to @@SECS@@ s) =="
i=0; st=""
while [ "$i" -lt @@TRIES@@ ]; do
  st=$(adbx get-state 2>/dev/null)
  case "$st" in recovery|device) break ;; esac
  st=""
  [ $((i % 10)) -eq 9 ] && echo "...still waiting"
  sleep @@PAUSE@@; i=$((i+1))
done
V=unknown
if [ -z "$st" ]; then
  echo "(the target didn't come back on adb in time - it may have rebooted by itself, or the recovery is still busy. Look at the target.)"
else
  [ "$LAST" -ge 0 ] && poll_log
  RAW=$(adbx shell "tail -n 1500 /tmp/recovery.log" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r')
  if [ -z "$RAW" ]; then
    echo "(could not read /tmp/recovery.log from the target - this recovery may not keep one)"
  else
    TOTAL=$(printf '%s\n' "$RAW" | wc -l)
    START=$(printf '%s\n' "$RAW" | grep -n -i -E 'installing zip file|starting adb sideload|starting sideload|update package|installing update|--update_package|starting installation' | grep -v -i 'error' | tail -n 1 | cut -d: -f1)
    if [ -z "$START" ]; then START=1; [ "$TOTAL" -gt 150 ] && START=$((TOTAL-149)); fi
    SEC=$(printf '%s\n' "$RAW" | tail -n +"$START")
    if [ "$LIVE_SHOWN" = 0 ]; then
      echo "== the recovery's own log of the install =="
      printf '%s\n' "$SEC" | tail -n 200 | while IFS= read -r l; do echo "[recovery] $l"; done
    fi
    OKP='script succeeded|Updater process ended with RC=0|complete \(status: 0\)|installation complete|install(ation)? successful|successfully installed|completed with status 0'
    ERRP='script aborted|Updater process ended with ERROR|Error installing zip|complete \(status: [1-9]|Installation aborted|install(ation)? failed|Error flashing|updater process ended with signal|E:Error in'
    SN=$(printf '%s\n' "$SEC" | grep -n -i -E "$OKP" | tail -n 1 | cut -d: -f1)
    EN=$(printf '%s\n' "$SEC" | grep -n -i -E "$ERRP" | tail -n 1 | cut -d: -f1)
    SN=${SN:-0}; EN=${EN:-0}
    if [ "$EN" -gt "$SN" ]; then V=err; elif [ "$SN" -gt 0 ]; then V=ok; fi
    echo "== key lines =="
    printf '%s\n' "$SEC" | grep -i -E '(^|[ ])E:|error|fail|abort|succe|complete|RC=' | tail -n 12 | while IFS= read -r l; do echo "[recovery] $l"; done
    case "$V" in
      ok) echo "== looks like the install SUCCEEDED (a guess from the recovery's log) ==" ;;
      err) echo "== the recovery reported an ERROR while installing (a guess from its log) ==" ;;
      *) echo "== couldn't tell from the log whether it worked - read the lines above ==" ;;
    esac
  fi
fi
if [ "$RC" -ne 0 ]; then exit "$RC"; fi
if [ "$V" = err ]; then exit 3; fi
exit 0
"""

_SIDELOAD_REBOOT = r"""
adbx reboot sideload
echo "Waiting for sideload mode..."
i=0; st=""
while [ "$i" -lt 40 ]; do st=$(adbx get-state 2>/dev/null); [ "$st" = "sideload" ] && break; sleep 1; i=$((i+1)); done
echo "state: $st"
[ "$st" = "sideload" ] || { echo "Target never entered sideload mode."; exit 3; }
"""


def sideload_script(adb, path, reboot=False, live=True, tries=75, pause=2):
    vals = {"A": adb, "ZIP": shq(path), "REBOOT": _SIDELOAD_REBOOT if reboot else "",
            "POLL": "poll_log" if live else ":", "TRIES": str(tries), "PAUSE": str(pause),
            "SECS": str(int(tries * pause))}
    return re.sub(r"@@(\w+)@@", lambda m: vals[m.group(1)], _SIDELOAD)


def dir_script(path):
    return ('cd %s 2>/dev/null || { echo E:cannot-open-folder; exit 0; }; '
            'for f in * .*; do [ "$f" = . ] && continue; [ "$f" = .. ] && continue; '
            '[ -e "$f" ] || continue; if [ -d "$f" ]; then echo D:"$f"; else echo F:"$f"; fi; done'
            ) % shq(path)


def parse_dir(out):
    items, err = [], ""
    for line in out.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("D:"):
            items.append((line[2:], True, None))
        elif line.startswith("F:"):
            items.append((line[2:], False, None))
        elif line.startswith("E:"):
            err = line[2:].replace("-", " ")
    if not items and not err and out.strip():
        err = out.strip().splitlines()[0]
    return items, err


def local_list(path):
    try:
        items = []
        with os.scandir(path) as it:
            for e in it:
                try:
                    d = e.is_dir()
                    size = None if d else e.stat().st_size
                except OSError:
                    d, size = False, None
                items.append((e.name, d, size))
        return items, ""
    except PermissionError:
        pass  # Android hides some folders from apps - root can still read them
    except OSError as e:
        return [], str(e)
    rc, out = su_run(dir_script(path), timeout=15)
    return parse_dir(out)

FLASH_PARTS = ["boot", "recovery", "vbmeta", "vbmeta_system", "dtbo", "vendor_boot",
               "init_boot", "system", "vendor", "product", "super", "userdata"]
RISKY = {"bootloader", "aboot", "abl", "xbl", "xbl_config", "sbl1", "tz", "hyp", "rpm",

GSI_NAMES = {  # mode -> (label used in picker titles, job title)
    "clean": ("Clean GSI", "GSI clean flash"),
    "dirty-system": ("Dirty flash (system)", "Dirty flash: system.img"),
    "dirty-super": ("Dirty flash (super)", "Dirty flash: super.img"),
}

# GSI flashing is three SEPARATE stages so each runs in the mode it needs, and the next one only
# starts after the previous one really worked:
#   1  vbmeta, in the bootloader (plain fastboot)       fastboot --disable-verification flash vbmeta vbmeta.img
#   2  reboot to fastbootd and wait until it answers "is-userspace: yes"
#   3  system (or super) in fastbootd: clean = delete product + flash system (frees more room and
#      retries if it runs out of space); dirty = just flash, nothing deleted
_GSI_COMMON = r"""
T=""; command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1 && T="timeout 10"
userspace() { $T @@FB@@ getvar is-userspace 2>&1 | grep -qi 'is-userspace: *yes'; }
# wait_mode userspace|bootloader. fastbootd answers "is-userspace: yes"; a listed device that doesn't
# is in the bootloader (many bootloaders answer FAILED to this variable rather than "no").
wait_mode() {
  i=0
  while [ "$i" -lt @@TRIES@@ ]; do
    if @@FB@@ devices 2>/dev/null | grep -qi fastboot; then
      if userspace; then
        [ "$1" = userspace ] && return 0
      else
        [ "$1" = bootloader ] && return 0
      fi
    fi
    [ $((i % 5)) -eq 4 ] && echo "...still waiting"
    sleep @@PAUSE@@; i=$((i+1))
  done
  return 1
}
"""

_GSI_S1 = r"""
V=@@V@@
echo "== step 1/3: vbmeta, in the bootloader =="
if userspace; then
  echo "The target is in fastbootd, but vbmeta has to be flashed from the bootloader. Going back there first..."
  @@FB@@ reboot bootloader || { echo "!! could not reboot to the bootloader - stopping."; exit 1; }
  sleep 5
  wait_mode bootloader || { echo "!! the target did not come back in the bootloader - stopping."; exit 1; }
  echo "bootloader is up."
fi
echo "+ @@FB@@ --disable-verification flash vbmeta $V"
@@FB@@ --disable-verification flash vbmeta "$V"
RC=$?
if [ "$RC" -ne 0 ]; then
  echo "!! vbmeta flash FAILED (exit $RC) - stopping here, nothing else was changed."
  exit "$RC"
fi
echo "== vbmeta flashed =="
exit 0
"""

_GSI_S2 = r"""
echo "== step 2/3: reboot to fastbootd =="
@@FB@@ reboot fastboot || { echo "!! the target refused to reboot to fastbootd - stopping."; exit 1; }
echo "waiting for it to come back as fastbootd (up to @@SECS@@ s)..."
sleep 5
if wait_mode userspace; then
  echo "fastbootd is up."
  exit 0
fi
echo "!! The target did not come back in fastbootd within @@SECS@@ s - stopping here."
echo "   Look at the target's screen. If it shows the bootloader, start the GSI flash again."
exit 1
"""

_GSI_S3_HEAD = r"""
IMG=@@IMG@@
TMPD="${TMPDIR:-/data/local/tmp}"
run_flash() {
  RCF="$TMPD/gsi_rc.$$"; LOG="$TMPD/gsi_log.$$"
  ( @@FB@@ flash "$1" "$2" 2>&1; echo $? > "$RCF" ) | tee "$LOG"
  RC=$(cat "$RCF" 2>/dev/null); [ -n "$RC" ] || RC=1
  rm -f "$RCF"
}
wait_mode userspace || { echo "!! The target is not in fastbootd, so nothing was flashed. Run the GSI flash again."; exit 1; }
"""

_GSI_CLEAN = _GSI_S3_HEAD + r"""
echo "== step 3/3: system, in fastbootd =="
echo "-- delete-logical-partition product --"
@@FB@@ delete-logical-partition product
echo "-- flash system (can take a while for a big GSI) --"
run_flash system "$IMG"
if [ "$RC" -ne 0 ] && grep -qi space "$LOG"; then
  echo "== not enough space - freeing more logical partitions =="
  for p in system_ext system_ext_a system_ext_b product product_a product_b; do
    echo "-- delete-logical-partition $p --"
    @@FB@@ delete-logical-partition "$p" || true
  done
  echo "== retrying system flash =="
  run_flash system "$IMG"
fi
rm -f "$LOG"
if [ "$RC" -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "== ALL DONE. Now on the device: open Recovery, then Factory reset. =="
else
  echo "!! system flash FAILED (exit $RC). Do not boot into it yet - see the messages above."
fi
exit "$RC"
"""

_GSI_DIRTY = _GSI_S3_HEAD + r"""
echo "== step 3/3: dirty flash @@PART@@, in fastbootd (your data is not touched) =="
run_flash @@PART@@ "$IMG"
if [ "$RC" -eq 0 ]; then
  rm -f "$LOG"
  echo "== ALL DONE. Reboot the device normally - nothing was deleted or wiped, no factory reset needed. =="
  exit 0
fi
if grep -qi space "$LOG"; then
  echo "!! Not enough room for this image. If it is a different ROM from the one installed, use the Clean flash instead."
fi
rm -f "$LOG"
echo "!! @@PART@@ flash FAILED (exit $RC). Do not boot into it yet - see the messages above."
exit "$RC"
"""


def gsi_script(stage, mode, vbmeta, image, fb="fastboot", tries=60, pause=2):
    """Shell script for one GSI stage (1 vbmeta / 2 fastbootd / 3 system|super). Single-pass token
    substitution so a file path can never be re-interpreted as a token."""
    dirty = mode != "clean"
    body = {1: _GSI_S1, 2: _GSI_S2, 3: _GSI_DIRTY if dirty else _GSI_CLEAN}[stage]
    vals = {"V": shq(vbmeta), "IMG": shq(image), "FB": fb, "TRIES": str(tries), "PAUSE": str(pause),
            "SECS": str(int(tries * pause)), "PART": "super" if mode == "dirty-super" else "system"}
    return re.sub(r"@@(\w+)@@", lambda m: vals[m.group(1)], _GSI_COMMON + body)


# ====================================================================== archives & files
# Plain Python (no screens): read zips/tars without extracting, safe extraction, file operations, zip
# creation, and in-place zip editing (delete / rename / move / add) that copies untouched entries RAW
# (no recompression), so editing a 1.5 GB ROM zip stays fast.
class ArchiveError(Exception):
    pass


class Cancelled(Exception):
    pass


def check_cancel(ctx):
    if ctx is not None and ctx.cancelled:
        raise Cancelled()


class TaskCtx:
    def __init__(self, job):
        self.job, self._last = job, 0.0

    @property
    def cancelled(self):
        return self.job.cancelled

    def log(self, text):
        self.job.q.put(text + "\n")

    def progress(self, done, total, label="", force=False):
        now = time.time()
        if not force and now - self._last < 0.35:
            return
        self._last = now
        pct = int(done * 100 / total) if total else 0
        self.job.q.put("  %3d%%   %s of %s   %s\n" % (pct, human(done), human(total), label[-60:]))


class TaskJob:
    """A Python function run in a thread, shown on the same live output screen as a Job."""
    root = False

    def __init__(self, title, fn, post=None):
        self.title, self.fn, self.post = title, fn, post
        self.q = queue.Queue()
        self.rc = None
        self.cancelled = self.cancel_started = self.ended = False
        self.proc = None
        self.script = ""
        self.token = "task%d" % next(_JOB_SEQ)

    def start(self):
        threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True).start()

    def cancel(self):
        if self.cancel_started:
            return False
        self.cancel_started = self.cancelled = True
        return True

    def _finish(self):
        if not self.ended:
            self.ended = True
            self.q.put(None)

    def _run(self):
        ctx = TaskCtx(self)
        try:
            rc = self.fn(ctx)
            self.rc = 0 if rc is None else rc
        except Cancelled:
            self.q.put("\nCancelled.\n")
            self.rc = -9
        except Exception as e:
            self.q.put("\n!! %s\n" % (e if isinstance(e, ArchiveError) else "".join(traceback.format_exception_only(type(e), e)).strip()))
            if not isinstance(e, ArchiveError):
                log("task failed: " + traceback.format_exc())
            self.rc = 1
        if self.post and not self.cancelled:
            try:
                self.q.put("\n" + self.post())
            except Exception as e:
                self.q.put("\n[follow-up failed: %s]\n" % e)
        self._finish()


# ---- names and paths ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def norm_name(name):
    n = name.replace("\x00", "")
    if "\\" in n and "/" not in n:  # zips made by some Windows tools use backslashes
        n = n.replace("\\", "/")
    return n


def safe_parts(name):
    """Path pieces of an archive member that are safe to create under a folder, else None ('..' = unsafe)."""
    parts = []
    for p in norm_name(name).split("/"):
        if p in ("", "."):
            continue
        if p == "..":
            return None
        parts.append(p)
    if parts and re.match(r"^[A-Za-z]:$", parts[0]):
        parts = parts[1:]
    return parts or None


_BAD_CHARS = re.compile(r'[\x00-\x1f:*?"<>|\\]')


def fix_component(c):
    """A version of one file name that any Android filesystem accepts (used only if the first try fails)."""
    c = _BAD_CHARS.sub("_", c).rstrip(" .") or "_"
    if len(c.encode("utf-8")) > 200:
        stem, ext = os.path.splitext(c)
        ext = ext[:20]
        keep = 200 - len(ext.encode("utf-8"))
        c = stem.encode("utf-8")[:keep].decode("utf-8", "ignore") + ext
    return c


def unique_path(path):
    if not os.path.lexists(path):
        return path
    base, ext = (path, "") if os.path.isdir(path) else os.path.splitext(path)
    n = 1
    while os.path.lexists("%s (%d)%s" % (base, n, ext)):
        n += 1
    return "%s (%d)%s" % (base, n, ext)


def tmp_beside(path):
    """A short temporary name in the same folder as `path` (can never be too long for the filesystem)."""
    return os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(path)), ".fk-" + os.urandom(4).hex() + ".tmp")


def free_space(path):
    p = os.path.abspath(path)
    while not os.path.exists(p):
        up = os.path.dirname(p)
        if up == p:
            break
        p = up
    return shutil.disk_usage(p).free


# ---- reading archives -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
ZIP_EXTS = (".zip", ".apk", ".jar", ".aar", ".xapk", ".apks", ".epub", ".docx", ".xlsx", ".pptx", ".odt", ".ods", ".kmz", ".whl")
TAR_EXTS = (".tar", ".tar.gz", ".tgz", ".tar.bz2", ".tbz2", ".tbz", ".tar.xz", ".txz", ".tar.lzma", ".tar.md5")
SINGLE_EXTS = (".gz", ".bz2", ".xz")
TEXT_EXTS = (".txt", ".log", ".prop", ".sh", ".xml", ".json", ".md", ".cfg", ".conf", ".ini", ".rc", ".py", ".csv", ".yml",
             ".yaml", ".html", ".htm", ".js", ".css", ".java", ".kt", ".c", ".h", ".cpp", ".mk", ".bp", ".te", ".fstab",
             ".policy", ".list", ".sha1", ".md5", ".sig")


def archive_kind(name):
    n = name.lower()
    if n.endswith(TAR_EXTS):
        return "tar"
    if n.endswith(ZIP_EXTS):
        return "zip"
    if n.endswith(SINGLE_EXTS):
        return "single"
    return None


def is_texty(name):
    n = name.lower()
    return n.endswith(TEXT_EXTS) or os.path.basename(n) in ("updater-script", "build.prop", "readme", "license")


class Entry:
    __slots__ = ("name", "dir", "size", "csize", "mtime", "enc", "crc", "raw", "link", "order")

    def __init__(self, name, is_dir, size=0, csize=0, mtime=None, enc=False, crc=0, raw=None, link=False, order=0):
        self.name, self.dir, self.size, self.csize, self.mtime = name, is_dir, size, csize, mtime
        self.enc, self.crc, self.raw, self.link, self.order = enc, crc, raw, link, order


class Archive:
    kind = "?"
    editable = False

    def __init__(self, path):
