```py
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ start up
def bootstrap():
    try:
        import termuxgui  # noqa: F401
    except ImportError:
        print("Installing the termuxgui Python package...")
        subprocess.call([sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install", "termuxgui"])
    if not (shutil.which("adb") and shutil.which("fastboot")):
        print("Installing android-tools (adb + fastboot)...")
        subprocess.call(["pkg", "install", "-y", "android-tools"])


def main():
    global tg
    bootstrap()
    try:
        import termuxgui as _tg
    except ImportError:
        print("Could not import termuxgui - run: pip install termuxgui")
        return 1
    tg = _tg
    try:
        conn = tg.Connection()
    except Exception as e:
        print("Could not connect to Termux:GUI (%s).\nInstall the Termux:GUI app from the same source as "
              "Termux itself (F-Droid or GitHub) and open it once." % e)
        return 1
    with conn as c:
        App(c).run()
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`get.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# One-line bootstrap:  curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/chakradhartode/flashkit/main/get.sh | bash
set -e
REPO="chakradhartode/flashkit"
DEST="${FLASHKIT_DIR:-$HOME/flashkit}"
command -v git >/dev/null 2>&1 || pkg install -y git
if [ -d "$DEST/.git" ]; then git -C "$DEST" pull --ff-only; else git clone --depth 1 "https://github.com/$REPO.git" "$DEST"; fi
exec bash "$DEST/install.sh" "$@"

```

`install.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# FlashKit installer - run it inside Termux:   bash install.sh
# Safe to run again any time. Flags: --update (quick refresh)  --no-plugins  --deps-only
set -u
DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
MODE=full; PLUGINS=1
for a in "$@"; do case "$a" in
  --update) MODE=update ;; --no-plugins) PLUGINS=0 ;; --deps-only) MODE=deps; PLUGINS=0 ;; esac; done
step() { printf '\n\033[1;36m==> %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }
ok()   { printf '\033[1;32m  ok\033[0m %s\n' "$*"; }
warn() { printf '\033[1;33m  !! %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }
die()  { printf '\033[1;31m!! %s\033[0m\n' "$*" >&2; exit 1; }

case "${PREFIX:-}" in *com.termux*) ;; *) die "This installs FlashKit into Termux - run it inside the Termux app." ;; esac
VERSION="$(cat "$DIR/VERSION" 2>/dev/null || echo '?')"
printf '\033[1mFlashKit %s\033[0m\n' "$VERSION"

step "Packages"
need=(python android-tools git curl termux-api dialog)
missing=()
for p in "${need[@]}"; do dpkg -s "$p" >/dev/null 2>&1 || missing+=("$p"); done
if [ "$MODE" = full ] || [ ${#missing[@]} -gt 0 ]; then
  pkg update -y >/dev/null 2>&1 || warn "pkg update had a problem (continuing - try termux-change-repo if installs fail)"
  if [ ${#missing[@]} -gt 0 ]; then
    pkg install -y "${missing[@]}" || die "Could not install: ${missing[*]}"
  fi
fi
ok "python, adb/fastboot, git, curl, termux-api, dialog"
dpkg -s termux-gui-package >/dev/null 2>&1 || pkg install -y termux-gui-package >/dev/null 2>&1 || true

step "Python library (termuxgui)"
if python -c "import termuxgui" 2>/dev/null; then
  [ "$MODE" = full ] && pip install --upgrade termuxgui >/dev/null 2>&1
  ok "termuxgui"
else
  pip install --upgrade termuxgui || die "pip could not install termuxgui"
  ok "termuxgui installed"
fi

if [ "$MODE" != deps ]; then
  step "Storage access"
  if [ -d "$HOME/storage/shared" ]; then ok "already allowed"; else
    termux-setup-storage
    for _ in $(seq 1 30); do [ -d "$HOME/storage/shared" ] && break; sleep 1; done
    [ -d "$HOME/storage/shared" ] && ok "allowed" || warn "Allow storage when Android asks, then run: flashkit setup"
  fi

  step "The flashkit command"
  chmod +x "$DIR/bin/flashkit" "$DIR/install.sh" "$DIR/tools/"*.sh 2>/dev/null
  git -C "$DIR" config core.fileMode false 2>/dev/null
  ln -sf "$DIR/bin/flashkit" "$PREFIX/bin/flashkit" && ok "type  flashkit  to open the FlashKit menu"

  step "Shortcuts"
  if [ ! -e "$PREFIX/bin/start" ] || grep -qs 'flashkit start' "$PREFIX/bin/start"; then
    cat > "$PREFIX/bin/start" <<'START'
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
# `start flashkit` -> opens the FlashKit app (written by the FlashKit installer)
case "${1:-}" in flashkit|FlashKit) shift; exec flashkit start "$@" ;; *) echo "usage: start flashkit"; exit 2 ;; esac
START
    chmod +x "$PREFIX/bin/start"; ok "start flashkit"
  else
    warn "another 'start' command already exists - use:  flashkit start"
  fi
  mkdir -p "$HOME/.shortcuts" && printf '#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash\nexec flashkit start\n' > "$HOME/.shortcuts/FlashKit" \
    && chmod +x "$HOME/.shortcuts/FlashKit" && ok "'FlashKit' entry for the Termux:Widget home-screen widget"
  bash "$DIR/bin/flashkit" hook && ok "after a Termux restart the FlashKit menu opens by itself (once)"
fi

if [ "$PLUGINS" = 1 ]; then
  step "Termux:GUI and Termux:API (matched to your Termux's signature)"
  mkdir -p "$HOME/.flashkit"; touch "$HOME/.flashkit/open-next"   # if Android restarts Termux while installing, the next session opens FlashKit
  python "$DIR/tools/termux_plugins.py" || warn "Plugins are not all set up - see the messages above, then run: flashkit plugins"
  rm -f "$HOME/.flashkit/open-next"
fi

printf '\n\033[1;32mDone.\033[0m  Open FlashKit with:  \033[1mflashkit\033[0m  (or  start flashkit)     Update later with:  \033[1mflashkit update\033[0m\n'
if [ "$MODE" = full ] && [ -t 0 ] && [ -t 1 ] && [ -z "${FLASHKIT_NO_OPEN:-}" ]; then
  printf 'Opening FlashKit...\n'; sleep 2; exec "$PREFIX/bin/flashkit"
fi

```

`legacy/flashkit.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# FlashKit - root adb/fastboot toolkit for Termux, dialog UI
# Run as: su -c sh /path/to/flashkit.sh

PREFIX=/data/data/com.termux/files/usr
HOME=/data/data/com.termux/files/home
TMPDIR="$PREFIX/tmp"
PATH="$PREFIX/bin:$PATH:/system/bin:/system/xbin:/sbin"
export PREFIX HOME TMPDIR PATH
mkdir -p "$TMPDIR" 2>/dev/null

if [ "$(id -u)" != "0" ]; then
  echo "Not root. Run it as: su -c sh $0"
  exit 1
fi

VERSION="v11"
STORAGE_ROOT="/storage/emulated/0"
[ -d "$STORAGE_ROOT" ] || STORAGE_ROOT="/sdcard"

# Dark theme: box background (blue) is deliberately different from the
# plain black screen behind it, so the dialog reads as a raised "card"
# instead of just outlined text on the same backdrop. Selected items/
# buttons use white text (not black) for contrast that holds up
# regardless of exactly how a given terminal renders ANSI magenta.
DIALOGRC="$TMPDIR/flashkit.dialogrc"
export DIALOGRC
cat > "$DIALOGRC" << 'RC_EOF'
use_shadow = ON
use_colors = ON
screen_color = (WHITE,BLACK,OFF)
shadow_color = (BLACK,BLACK,ON)
dialog_color = (WHITE,BLUE,OFF)
title_color = (MAGENTA,BLUE,ON)
border_color = (MAGENTA,BLUE,OFF)
border2_color = (MAGENTA,BLUE,OFF)
button_active_color = (WHITE,MAGENTA,ON)
button_inactive_color = (WHITE,BLUE,OFF)
button_key_active_color = (WHITE,MAGENTA,ON)
button_key_inactive_color = (MAGENTA,BLUE,ON)
button_label_active_color = (WHITE,MAGENTA,ON)
button_label_inactive_color = (WHITE,BLUE,OFF)
inputbox_color = (WHITE,BLUE,OFF)
inputbox_border_color = (CYAN,BLUE,OFF)
inputbox_border2_color = (CYAN,BLUE,OFF)
menubox_color = (WHITE,BLUE,OFF)
menubox_border_color = (MAGENTA,BLUE,OFF)
menubox_border2_color = (MAGENTA,BLUE,OFF)
item_color = (WHITE,BLUE,OFF)
item_selected_color = (WHITE,MAGENTA,ON)
tag_color = (CYAN,BLUE,ON)
tag_selected_color = (WHITE,MAGENTA,ON)
tag_key_color = (CYAN,BLUE,ON)
tag_key_selected_color = (WHITE,MAGENTA,ON)
position_indicator_color = (CYAN,BLUE,ON)
uarrow_color = (CYAN,BLUE,ON)
darrow_color = (CYAN,BLUE,ON)
RC_EOF

# Size dialog boxes against the ACTUAL terminal, not dialog's own "0 0"
# auto-size. Recomputed every call in case the on-screen keyboard
# opening/closing changed the visible rows.
dsize() {
  sz=$(stty size 2>/dev/null)
  rows=${sz%% *}
  cols=${sz##* }
  case "$rows" in ''|*[!0-9]*) rows=30 ;; esac
  case "$cols" in ''|*[!0-9]*) cols=50 ;; esac
  DH=$((rows - 2)); [ "$DH" -lt 10 ] && DH=10
  DW=$((cols - 2)); [ "$DW" -lt 30 ] && DW=30
  MH=$((DH - 8)); [ "$MH" -lt 3 ] && MH=3
}

HAVE_TIMEOUT=0
command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1 && HAVE_TIMEOUT=1

# Runs a command entirely inside the themed dialog UI: a "please wait"
# box while it runs, then its captured output (plus exit code) in a
# scrollable box you dismiss with OK - no more dropping to plain
# unstyled terminal text for the actual flashing/pushing/etc.
run_boxed() {
  title="$1"; secs="$2"; shift 2
  dsize
  dialog --title "$title" --infobox "Working, please wait..." "$DH" "$DW"
  outfile="$TMPDIR/flashkit_out"
  if [ "$HAVE_TIMEOUT" = "1" ]; then
    timeout "$secs" "$@" > "$outfile" 2>&1
  else
    "$@" > "$outfile" 2>&1
  fi
  rc=$?
  {
    echo ""
    if [ "$rc" = "0" ]; then echo "[done - exit code 0]"; else echo "[exit code $rc]"; fi
  } >> "$outfile"
  dsize
  dialog --title "$title" --textbox "$outfile" "$DH" "$DW"
}

install_deps() {
  clear
  echo "FlashKit $VERSION - checking packages (adb, fastboot, dialog)..."
  pkg update -y >/dev/null 2>&1
  pkg install -y android-tools dialog >/dev/null 2>&1
  if command -v adb >/dev/null 2>&1 && command -v fastboot >/dev/null 2>&1 && command -v dialog >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "OK - adb, fastboot and dialog are all present."
  else
    echo "WARNING: something is still missing after install (check your internet), continuing anyway..."
    sleep 2
  fi
}

# Lightweight auto-fix: reinstalls adb/fastboot if missing, clears a
# stuck server, and re-opens USB node permissions.
fix_adb() {
  if ! command -v adb >/dev/null 2>&1 || ! command -v fastboot >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    pkg install -y android-tools >/dev/null 2>&1
  fi
  adb kill-server >/dev/null 2>&1
  rm -f "$HOME"/.android/adb*.lock 2>/dev/null
  chmod -R 666 /dev/bus/usb/*/* 2>/dev/null
  adb start-server >/dev/null 2>&1
  sleep 1
}

info() { dsize; dialog --title "$1" --msgbox "$2" "$DH" "$DW"; }
# Big full-width Yes/No rows instead of dialog's tiny "< OK >  <Cancel>".
yesno() {
  dsize
  r=$(dialog --title "Confirm" --menu "$1" "$DH" "$DW" 4 \
    yes "Yes, continue" no "No, cancel" \
    3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)
  [ "$r" = "yes" ]
}
inputbox() { dsize; dialog --title "$1" --inputbox "$2" "$DH" "$DW" "$3" 3>&1 1>&2 2>&3; }

# Own tap-friendly file browser (numbered --menu) instead of dialog's
# --fselect, which only updates its path box on keyboard navigation.
# Confined to $STORAGE_ROOT and below. Always has a big "Cancel" row of
# its own, not just the small Cancel button.
browse_pick() {
  dir="$1"
  title="$2"
  TAB="$(printf '\t')"
  while true; do
    map="$TMPDIR/flashkit_map"
    : > "$map"
    n=0
    set --
    if [ "$dir" != "$STORAGE_ROOT" ]; then
      n=$((n+1))
      printf '%s%s%s\n' "$n" "$TAB" "$(dirname "$dir")" >> "$map"
      set -- "$@" "$n" ".. (up)"
    fi
    for entry in "$dir"/*/; do
      [ -d "$entry" ] || continue
      name="${entry%/}"; name="${name##*/}"
      n=$((n+1))
      printf '%s%s%s\n' "$n" "$TAB" "$entry" >> "$map"
      set -- "$@" "$n" "$name/"
    done
    for entry in "$dir"/*; do
      [ -f "$entry" ] || continue
      name="${entry##*/}"
      n=$((n+1))
      printf '%s%s%s\n' "$n" "$TAB" "$entry" >> "$map"
      set -- "$@" "$n" "$name"
    done
    set -- "$@" 0 "Type a path instead" C "Cancel / back"
    dsize
    sel=$(dialog --title "$title" --menu "$dir" "$DH" "$DW" "$MH" "$@" 3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)
    if [ -z "$sel" ] || [ "$sel" = "C" ]; then
      rm -f "$map"
      return 1
    fi
    if [ "$sel" = "0" ]; then
      rm -f "$map"
      p=$(inputbox "$title" "Full path:" "$dir/")
      [ -z "$p" ] && return 1
      if [ -f "$p" ]; then echo "$p"; return 0; fi
      if [ -d "$p" ]; then dir="${p%/}"; continue; fi
      return 1
    fi
    picked=$(grep "^${sel}${TAB}" "$map" | cut -f2-)
    rm -f "$map"
    if [ -d "$picked" ]; then
      dir="${picked%/}"
    else
      echo "$picked"
      return 0
    fi
  done
}

pick_file() {
  browse_pick "$STORAGE_ROOT" "$1"
}

adb_state() {
  adb devices 2>/dev/null | grep -v "^List of devices" | grep -v "^$" | head -n1 | cut -f2
}

fastboot_serial() {
  fastboot devices 2>/dev/null | head -n1 | cut -f1
}

adb_present() {
  s=$(adb_state)
  if [ -z "$s" ]; then
    fix_adb
    s=$(adb_state)
  fi
  echo "$s"
}

ensure_adb_device() {
  s=$(adb_state)
  if [ "$s" != "device" ]; then
    fix_adb
    s=$(adb_state)
  fi
  [ "$s" = "device" ]
}

ensure_fastboot_device() {
  d=$(fastboot_serial)
  if [ -z "$d" ]; then
    fix_adb
    d=$(fastboot_serial)
  fi
  [ -n "$d" ]
}

# Lists a directory ON THE CONNECTED DEVICE via adb shell (D:name / F:name).
remote_ls() {
  rdir="$1"
  qdir=$(printf '%s' "$rdir" | sed "s/'/'\\\\''/g")
  adb shell "cd '$qdir' 2>/dev/null && for f in * .*; do [ \"\$f\" = '.' ] && continue; [ \"\$f\" = '..' ] && continue; [ -e \"\$f\" ] || continue; if [ -d \"\$f\" ]; then echo D:\"\$f\"; else echo F:\"\$f\"; fi; done" 2>/dev/null
}

# Same browsing UX as browse_pick, against the device's own filesystem.
# mode=file returns on landing on a file; mode=dir also lets you confirm
# whichever folder you're sitting in. Confined to its starting root.
remote_browse() {
  dir="$1"
  title="$2"
  mode="$3"
  root="$dir"
  TAB="$(printf '\t')"
  while true; do
    map="$TMPDIR/flashkit_rmap"
    : > "$map"
    n=0
    set --
    if [ "$dir" != "$root" ]; then
      n=$((n+1))
      printf '%s%s%s\n' "$n" "$TAB" "$(dirname "$dir")" >> "$map"
      set -- "$@" "$n" ".. (up)"
    fi
    while IFS= read -r line; do
      case "$line" in
        D:*|F:*) : ;;
        *) continue ;;
      esac
      kind="${line%%:*}"
      name="${line#*:}"
      n=$((n+1))
      case "$dir" in
        */) full="${dir}${name}" ;;
        *) full="${dir}/${name}" ;;
      esac
      printf '%s%s%s%s%s\n' "$n" "$TAB" "$full" "$TAB" "$kind" >> "$map"
      if [ "$kind" = "D" ]; then
        set -- "$@" "$n" "$name/"
      else
        set -- "$@" "$n" "$name"
      fi
    done <<REMOTE_EOF
$(remote_ls "$dir")
REMOTE_EOF
    if [ "$mode" = "dir" ]; then
      set -- "$@" U "[use this folder]"
    fi
    set -- "$@" 0 "Type a path instead" C "Cancel / back"
    dsize
    sel=$(dialog --title "$title" --menu "$dir (on device)" "$DH" "$DW" "$MH" "$@" 3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)
    if [ -z "$sel" ] || [ "$sel" = "C" ]; then
      rm -f "$map"
      return 1
    fi
    if [ "$sel" = "U" ]; then
      rm -f "$map"
      echo "$dir"
      return 0
    fi
    if [ "$sel" = "0" ]; then
      rm -f "$map"
      p=$(inputbox "$title" "Full path on device:" "$dir/")
      [ -z "$p" ] && return 1
      echo "$p"
      return 0
    fi
    row=$(grep "^${sel}${TAB}" "$map")
    rm -f "$map"
    rpath=$(printf '%s' "$row" | cut -f2)
    rkind=$(printf '%s' "$row" | cut -f3)
    if [ "$rkind" = "D" ]; then
      dir="$rpath"
    else
      echo "$rpath"
      return 0
    fi
  done
}

act_flash() {
  if ! ensure_fastboot_device; then
    info "No device" "No fastboot device found.

Check the OTG cable/adapter and that the target is in bootloader mode."
    return
  fi
  dsize
  part=$(dialog --title "Partition" --menu "Pick a partition:" "$DH" "$DW" "$MH" \
    boot "" recovery "" vbmeta "" vbmeta_a "" vbmeta_b "" system "" vendor "" dtbo "" other "type a name" \
    3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)
  [ -z "$part" ] && return
  if [ "$part" = "other" ]; then
    part=$(inputbox "Partition" "Partition name:" "")
    [ -z "$part" ] && return
  fi
  f=$(pick_file "Image for $part")
  [ -z "$f" ] && return
  yesno "Flash this file:
$f

to partition: $part ?" || return
  run_boxed "Flashing $part" 300 fastboot flash "$part" "$f"
}

act_sideload() {
  f=$(pick_file "ROM/update zip")
  [ -z "$f" ] && return
  s=$(adb_state)
  if [ "$s" != "sideload" ] && [ "$s" != "device" ]; then
    fix_adb
    s=$(adb_state)
  fi
  case "$s" in
    sideload) : ;;
    device)
      yesno "Reboot target to recovery and sideload:
$f" || return
      dsize
      dialog --title "Sideload" --infobox "Rebooting to sideload mode..." "$DH" "$DW"
      adb reboot sideload >/dev/null 2>&1
      sleep 5
      ;;
    *)
      info "No device" "adb state: ${s:-none}

Put the target at 'Apply update > Apply from ADB' in recovery, or boot it normally with USB debugging authorized, then retry."
      return
      ;;
  esac
  run_boxed "Sideloading $(basename "$f")" 300 adb sideload "$f"
}

act_install() {
  if ! ensure_adb_device; then
    info "No device" "No adb device found.

Check the OTG cable/adapter and USB-debugging authorization on the target."
    return
  fi
  f=$(pick_file "APK to install")
  [ -z "$f" ] && return
  run_boxed "Installing $(basename "$f")" 120 adb install "$f"
}

act_reboot() {
  s=$(adb_state)
  d=$(fastboot_serial)
  if [ -z "$s" ] && [ -z "$d" ]; then
    fix_adb
    s=$(adb_state)
    d=$(fastboot_serial)
  fi
  dsize
  case "$s" in
    device|recovery|sideload)
      t=$(dialog --title "Reboot (adb - $s)" --menu "Reboot target to:" "$DH" "$DW" "$MH" \
        system "" bootloader "" recovery "" sideload "" \
        3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)
      [ -z "$t" ] && return
      if [ "$t" = "system" ]; then run_boxed "Rebooting" 20 adb reboot; else run_boxed "Rebooting to $t" 20 adb reboot "$t"; fi
      ;;
    *)
      if [ -n "$d" ]; then
        t=$(dialog --title "Reboot (fastboot)" --menu "Reboot target to:" "$DH" "$DW" "$MH" \
          system "" bootloader "" recovery "" fastbootd "" \
          3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)
        [ -z "$t" ] && return
        case "$t" in
          system) run_boxed "Rebooting" 20 fastboot reboot ;;
          fastbootd) run_boxed "Rebooting to fastbootd" 20 fastboot reboot fastboot ;;
          *) run_boxed "Rebooting to $t" 20 fastboot reboot "$t" ;;
        esac
      else
        info "No device" "adb state: ${s:-none}, no fastboot device either."
      fi
      ;;
  esac
}

# Three separate gates before anything gets erased: two escalating
# yes/no warnings, then a type-the-exact-name confirmation.
act_erase() {
  if ! ensure_fastboot_device; then
    info "No device" "No fastboot device found."
    return
  fi
  part=$(inputbox "Erase" "Partition to ERASE:" "")
  [ -z "$part" ] && return
  yesno "WARNING 1 of 3

You are about to ERASE the '$part' partition.

This cannot be undone." || return
  yesno "WARNING 2 of 3

Erasing '$part' can leave this device unable to boot unless you reflash it right after.

Still continue?" || return
  confirm_text=$(inputbox "WARNING 3 of 3" "Type the partition name exactly to confirm:" "")
  if [ "$confirm_text" != "$part" ]; then
    info "Cancelled" "Text didn't match '$part' - erase cancelled, nothing was touched."
    return
  fi
  run_boxed "Erasing $part" 60 fastboot erase "$part"
}

# Stays in the picked tool (adb or fastboot) after running a command,
# looping back to the same command list instead of dropping to the main
# menu - pick "Back" when you're actually done.
act_raw() {
  dsize
  tool=$(dialog --title "Raw command" --menu "Tool:" "$DH" "$DW" "$MH" adb "" fastboot "" 3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)
  [ -z "$tool" ] && return
  while true; do
    dsize
    if [ "$tool" = "fastboot" ]; then
      cmdline=$(dialog --title "fastboot command" --menu "Pick one (or Custom):" "$DH" "$DW" "$MH" \
        "devices" "list connected devices" \
        "getvar all" "show all bootloader variables" \
        "flashing unlock" "unlock bootloader (newer devices)" \
        "flashing lock" "lock bootloader (newer devices)" \
        "flashing unlock_critical" "unlock critical partitions" \
        "oem unlock" "unlock bootloader (older devices)" \
        "oem lock" "lock bootloader (older devices)" \
        "continue" "resume normal boot" \
        "reboot" "reboot to system" \
        "reboot bootloader" "reboot to bootloader" \
        "reboot recovery" "reboot to recovery" \
        "reboot fastboot" "reboot to fastbootd" \
        "set_active a" "switch active slot to A" \
        "set_active b" "switch active slot to B" \
        "Custom" "type your own command" \
        "Back" "back to main menu" \
        3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)
    else
      cmdline=$(dialog --title "adb command" --menu "Pick one (or Custom):" "$DH" "$DW" "$MH" \
        "devices" "list connected devices" \
        "shell getprop ro.build.version.release" "Android version" \
        "shell getprop ro.product.model" "device model" \
        "reboot" "reboot to system" \
        "reboot bootloader" "reboot to bootloader" \
        "reboot recovery" "reboot to recovery" \
        "reboot sideload" "reboot to sideload" \
        "wait-for-device" "block until a device shows up" \
        "Custom" "type your own command" \
        "Back" "back to main menu" \
        3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)
    fi
    [ -z "$cmdline" ] && return
    [ "$cmdline" = "Back" ] && return
    if [ "$cmdline" = "Custom" ]; then
      cmdline=$(inputbox "Raw $tool command" "Command, no '$tool' prefix:" "")
      [ -z "$cmdline" ] && continue
    fi
    if [ "$tool" = "adb" ]; then
      s=$(adb_present)
      case "$s" in
        device|recovery|sideload) : ;;
        "")
          info "No device" "No adb device found at all.

Check the OTG cable/adapter."
          continue
          ;;
        *)
          info "Not ready" "adb state: $s

If that says unauthorized, accept the USB-debugging prompt on the target's screen."
          continue
          ;;
      esac
    else
      ensure_fastboot_device || { info "No device" "No fastboot device found."; continue; }
    fi
    run_boxed "Running: $tool $cmdline" 300 "$tool" $cmdline
  done
}

# Browse the connected device's own storage, and push/pull files between
# it and this phone.
act_devfiles() {
  s=$(adb_present)
  case "$s" in
    device|recovery) : ;;
    *)
      info "No device" "adb state: ${s:-none}

Need a normal or recovery adb connection to browse the device's files."
      return
      ;;
  esac
  while true; do
    dsize
    op=$(dialog --title "Device files" --menu "What do you want to do?" "$DH" "$DW" "$MH" \
      push "Copy a file from THIS phone TO the device" \
      pull "Copy a file from the device TO this phone" \
      browse "Just look around on the device" \
      back "Back to main menu" \
      3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)
    case "$op" in
      push)
        f=$(pick_file "File to push to the device")
        [ -z "$f" ] && continue
        dest=$(remote_browse "/sdcard" "Pick a destination FOLDER on the device" "dir")
        [ -z "$dest" ] && continue
        remote_target="${dest%/}/$(basename "$f")"
        yesno "Push:
$f

to the device at:
$remote_target ?" || continue
        run_boxed "Pushing $(basename "$f")" 300 adb push "$f" "$remote_target"
        ;;
      pull)
        rf=$(remote_browse "/sdcard" "Pick a file on the device to pull" "file")
        [ -z "$rf" ] && continue
        local_dest="$STORAGE_ROOT/Download/$(basename "$rf")"
        yesno "Pull:
$rf

from the device, saving to:
$local_dest ?" || continue
        mkdir -p "$STORAGE_ROOT/Download" 2>/dev/null
        run_boxed "Pulling $(basename "$rf")" 300 adb pull "$rf" "$local_dest"
        ;;
      browse)
        remote_browse "/sdcard" "Device storage" "dir" >/dev/null
        ;;
      back|"") return ;;
    esac
  done
}

act_list() {
  out="-- adb --
$(adb devices 2>/dev/null)

-- fastboot --
$(fastboot devices 2>/dev/null)"
  info "Connected devices" "$out"
}

# Full diagnostic pass: binaries, raw USB enumeration, adb state, fastboot
# state, all in one report.
act_fix() {
  msg=""
  if command -v adb >/dev/null 2>&1 && command -v fastboot >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    msg="adb/fastboot: installed"
  else
    msg="adb/fastboot: was missing, reinstalling now"
  fi
  fix_adb
  usb="$(ls /dev/bus/usb/*/* 2>/dev/null)"
  if [ -z "$usb" ]; then
    msg="$msg
USB: nothing on the OTG port at all - try a different cable/adapter"
  else
    msg="$msg
USB nodes: $usb"
  fi
  s=$(adb_state)
  d=$(fastboot_serial)
  msg="$msg
adb state: ${s:-none}
fastboot device: ${d:-none}"
  info "Diagnostics" "$msg"
}

main_menu() {
  dsize
  dialog --backtitle "FlashKit $VERSION" --title "Main menu" --menu "Choose an action:" "$DH" "$DW" "$MH" \
    1 "Flash a partition/image (fastboot)" \
    2 "Sideload a ROM/zip (adb)" \
    3 "Install an APK (adb)" \
    4 "Reboot target" \
    5 "Erase a partition [destructive]" \
    6 "Raw adb/fastboot command" \
    7 "Fix ADB/USB detection" \
    8 "List connected devices" \
    9 "Device files (push/pull/browse)" \
    0 "Exit" \
    3>&1 1>&2 2>&3
}

install_deps
fix_adb
while true; do
  choice=$(main_menu)
  case "$choice" in
    1) act_flash ;;
    2) act_sideload ;;
    3) act_install ;;
    4) act_reboot ;;
    5) act_erase ;;
    6) act_raw ;;
    7) act_fix ;;
    8) act_list ;;
    9) act_devfiles ;;
    0|"") clear; stty sane 2>/dev/null; exit 0 ;;
    *) : ;;
  esac
done
```

`tests/test_docs.py`:

```py
"""The README must not point at missing images or pages; every SVG must be valid; the social image must fit GitHub's limits."""
import glob, os, re, struct, unittest, xml.dom.minidom
ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
def read(p): return open(os.path.join(ROOT, p), encoding="utf-8").read()

class Docs(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_readme_links_exist(self):
        text = read("README.md")
        refs = set(re.findall(r'src="([^"#:]+)"', text)) | set(re.findall(r'\]\(((?:assets|docs)/[^)#]+|LICENSE|CHANGELOG\.md)\)', text))
        self.assertTrue(refs)
        for r in refs: self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(ROOT, r)), "missing: " + r)
    def test_svgs_are_valid_and_clean(self):
        files = glob.glob(os.path.join(ROOT, "assets", "*.svg")); self.assertGreaterEqual(len(files), 5)
        for f in files:
            raw = open(f, "rb").read(); xml.dom.minidom.parseString(raw)
            self.assertTrue(raw.startswith(b"<?xml"), os.path.basename(f)); self.assertNotIn("\u00c2".encode("utf-8"), raw, "garbled character in " + f)
            self.assertNotIn(b"<script", raw)
    def test_social_preview(self):
        p = os.path.join(ROOT, "assets", "social-preview.png"); b = open(p, "rb").read()
        self.assertEqual(b[:8], b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"); self.assertEqual(struct.unpack(">II", b[16:24]), (1280, 640)); self.assertLess(len(b), 1024 * 1024)
    def test_placeholder_only_where_publish_fills_it(self):
        allowed = {"README.md", "docs/GUIDE.md", "get.sh", "tools/repo.conf", ".github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/config.yml", "tools/publish.sh", "bin/flashkit"}
        for dp, _, fs in os.walk(ROOT):
            if ".git" in dp.split(os.sep) or "__pycache__" in dp: continue
            for f in fs:
                rel = os.path.relpath(os.path.join(dp, f), ROOT)
                if rel.endswith((".png", ".pyc")) or rel.startswith("tests"): continue
                if "__GH_REPO__" in open(os.path.join(dp, f), encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").read(): self.assertIn(rel, allowed, rel)
    def test_publish_fills_every_file_that_has_the_placeholder(self):
        pub = read("tools/publish.sh"); m = re.search(r"for f in ([^;]+); do", pub); self.assertTrue(m)
        filled = set(m.group(1).split())
        for rel in ("README.md", "docs/GUIDE.md", "get.sh", "tools/repo.conf", ".github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/config.yml"): self.assertIn(rel, filled)

    def test_repo_version_is_the_apps_version(self):
        """Docs/graphics-only changes must not change the version: it only moves when the app does."""
        app = re.search(r'^VERSION = "([^"]+)"', read("flashkit_gui.py"), re.M).group(1)
        self.assertEqual(read("VERSION").strip(), app)
    def test_version_badge_comes_from_the_version_file(self):
        # Checked out from GitHub, README.md already has publish.sh's sed applied (the placeholder is gone,
        # replaced with the real version) - checked out from the shipped zip, it still says __VERSION__. Both
        # are correct; what must hold either way is that the badge isn't showing some OTHER, unrelated text.
        m = re.search(r"img\.shields\.io/badge/version-([^-?]+)-9B6BFF", read("README.md"))
        self.assertIsNotNone(m, "version badge not found in README.md")
        self.assertIn(m.group(1), ("__VERSION__", read("VERSION").strip()))
        pub = read("tools/publish.sh"); self.assertIn("__VERSION__", pub); self.assertNotIn("git tag -f", pub)

if __name__ == "__main__": unittest.main()
```

`tests/test_plugins.py`:

```py
"""Tests for tools/termux_plugins.py with fake `pm`/`su`, a local fake GitHub + F-Droid, and real openssl certificates."""
import hashlib, http.server, json, os, shutil, stat, struct, subprocess, sys, tempfile, threading, unittest, zipfile
ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
SCRIPT = os.path.join(ROOT, "tools", "termux_plugins.py")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "tools"))
import termux_plugins as tp

def sh(cmd): return subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, check=True, capture_output=True)

class Key:
    def __init__(self, tmp, name):
        self.tmp, self.name = tmp, name
        sh("openssl req -x509 -newkey rsa:2048 -nodes -keyout %s/%s.key -out %s/%s.pem -subj /CN=%s -days 3650" % (tmp, name, tmp, name, name))
        self.der = subprocess.run(["openssl", "x509", "-in", "%s/%s.pem" % (tmp, name), "-outform", "DER"], capture_output=True, check=True).stdout
        self.fp = hashlib.sha256(self.der).hexdigest()
        open(tmp + "/data.bin", "wb").write(b"signed content")
        self.p7 = subprocess.run(["openssl", "smime", "-sign", "-binary", "-in", tmp + "/data.bin", "-signer", "%s/%s.pem" % (tmp, name),
                                  "-inkey", "%s/%s.key" % (tmp, name), "-outform", "DER"], capture_output=True, check=True).stdout

def lp(b): return struct.pack("<I", len(b)) + b

def make_apk(path, pkg, key, scheme="v1"):
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path, "w") as z:
        z.writestr("AndroidManifest.xml", b"manifest"); z.writestr("fake_package.txt", pkg); z.writestr("fake_key.txt", key.name if key else "none")
        if key and scheme in ("v1", "both"): z.writestr("META-INF/CERT.RSA", key.p7)
    if key and scheme in ("v2", "both"):
        data = open(path, "rb").read()
        i = data.rfind(b"PK\x05\x06"); cd = struct.unpack("<I", data[i + 16:i + 20])[0]
        signed = lp(b"") + lp(lp(key.der))
        value = lp(lp(lp(signed) + lp(b"") + lp(b"")))
        pairs = struct.pack("<Q", 4 + len(value)) + struct.pack("<I", 0x7109871a) + value
        block = struct.pack("<Q", len(pairs) + 24) + pairs + struct.pack("<Q", len(pairs) + 24) + b"APK Sig Block 42"
        data = data[:cd] + block + data[cd:]
        j = data.rfind(b"PK\x05\x06"); data = data[:j + 16] + struct.pack("<I", cd + len(block)) + data[j + 20:]
        open(path, "wb").write(data)

FAKE_PM = r'''#!/bin/sh
S="$FKSTATE"
blind() { [ -z "$FKROOT" ] && [ -f "$S/blind" ]; }
case "$1" in
 list) if blind; then echo "package:com.termux  installer=null"; else while read -r p i; do echo "package:$p  installer=$i"; done < "$S/packages"; fi ;;
 path) if blind && [ "$2" != com.termux ]; then exit 0; fi; grep "^$2 " "$S/paths" | cut -d' ' -f2 | sed 's/^/package:/' ;;
 install) f="$4"; [ -f "$f" ] || { echo "Failure [no file]"; exit 1; }; p=$(unzip -p "$f" fake_package.txt); k=$(unzip -p "$f" fake_key.txt); mkdir -p "$S/apks" "$S/keys"
   if [ "$p" != com.termux ] && [ -f "$S/keys/com.termux" ] && [ "$k" != "$(cat "$S/keys/com.termux")" ]; then echo "Failure [INSTALL_FAILED_SHARED_USER_INCOMPATIBLE: signatures do not match]"; exit 1; fi
   if [ -f "$S/keys/$p" ] && [ "$k" != "$(cat "$S/keys/$p")" ]; then echo "Failure [INSTALL_FAILED_UPDATE_INCOMPATIBLE: Package $p signatures do not match previously installed version; ignoring!]"; exit 1; fi
   cp "$f" "$S/apks/$p.apk"; echo "$k" > "$S/keys/$p"
   grep -v "^$p " "$S/packages" > "$S/p.tmp"; echo "$p null" >> "$S/p.tmp"; mv "$S/p.tmp" "$S/packages"
   grep -v "^$p " "$S/paths" > "$S/q.tmp"; echo "$p $S/apks/$p.apk" >> "$S/q.tmp"; mv "$S/q.tmp" "$S/paths"; echo "$p" >> "$S/installed.log"; echo Success ;;
 uninstall) grep -v "^$2 " "$S/packages" > "$S/p.tmp"; mv "$S/p.tmp" "$S/packages"; rm -f "$S/keys/$2"; echo "$2" >> "$S/uninstalled.log"; echo Success ;;
esac
'''
FAKE_SU = r'''#!/bin/sh
[ "$1" = "-c" ] || exit 1
export FKROOT=1
case "$2" in *"id -u") echo 0 ;; *) mkdir -p "$FKSTATE/tmp"; exec sh -c "$(printf '%s' "$2" | sed "s#/data/local/tmp#$FKSTATE/tmp#g")" ;; esac
'''
FAKE_OPEN = '#!/bin/sh\necho "$1" >> "$FKSTATE/open.log"\nFKROOT=1 pm install -r -d "$1" >> "$FKSTATE/open-out.log" 2>&1\n'

class Env:
    def __init__(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="fkt_"); self.home = self.tmp + "/home"; self.state = self.tmp + "/state"; self.bin = self.tmp + "/bin"
        for d in (self.home, self.state, self.bin, self.tmp + "/apks"): os.makedirs(d)
        for name, body in (("pm", FAKE_PM), ("su", FAKE_SU), ("termux-open", FAKE_OPEN), ("appops", '#!/bin/sh\necho "$@" >> "$FKSTATE/appops.log"\n'), ("am", '#!/bin/sh\necho "$@" >> "$FKSTATE/am.log"\n'), ("getprop", "#!/bin/sh\necho arm64-v8a\n")):
            open(self.bin + "/" + name, "w").write(body); os.chmod(self.bin + "/" + name, 0o755)
        self.keys = {}; self.requests = []; self.routes = {}
        open(self.state + "/packages", "w").close(); open(self.state + "/paths", "w").close(); os.makedirs(self.state + "/keys")
        env = self
        class H(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def log_message(self, *a): pass
            def do_GET(self):
                env.requests.append(self.path)
                r = env.routes.get(self.path)
                if r is None: self.send_response(404); self.end_headers(); return
                self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(r))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(r)
        self.srv = http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), H); self.port = self.srv.server_address[1]
        threading.Thread(target=self.srv.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
    def key(self, n):
        if n not in self.keys: self.keys[n] = Key(self.tmp, n)
        return self.keys[n]
    def install_pkg(self, pkg, key, installer="null", scheme="v1", real_key=None):
        p = "%s/apks/%s.apk" % (self.tmp, pkg); make_apk(p, pkg, self.key(key) if key else None, scheme)
        os.makedirs(self.state + "/keys", exist_ok=True); open(self.state + "/keys/" + pkg, "w").write(real_key or key or "none")
        for f in ("packages", "paths"):
            lines = [l for l in open(self.state + "/" + f).read().splitlines() if not l.startswith(pkg + " ")]
            lines.append("%s %s" % (pkg, installer if f == "packages" else p)); open(self.state + "/" + f, "w").write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    def github(self, repo, assets):  # assets: [(name, key, digest)]
        out = []
        for name, key, digest in assets:
            make_apk(self.tmp + "/apks/dl_" + name, {"termux/termux-gui": "com.termux.gui", "termux/termux-api": "com.termux.api"}[repo], self.key(key)); self.routes["/dl/" + name] = open(self.tmp + "/apks/dl_" + name, "rb").read()
            a = {"name": name, "browser_download_url": "http://127.0.0.1:%d/dl/%s" % (self.port, name)}
            if digest: a["digest"] = digest
            out.append(a)
        self.routes["/repos/%s/releases/latest" % repo] = json.dumps({"tag_name": "v1", "assets": out}).encode()
    def fdroid(self, pkg, key):
        make_apk(self.tmp + "/apks/fd_" + pkg, pkg, self.key(key)); self.routes["/fdroid-repo/%s_7.apk" % pkg] = open(self.tmp + "/apks/fd_" + pkg, "rb").read()
        self.routes["/fdroid-api/" + pkg] = json.dumps({"packageName": pkg, "suggestedVersionCode": 7, "packages": [{"versionCode": 7}]}).encode()
    def run(self, *args, **extra):
        e = dict(os.environ, **extra); e.update(HOME=self.home, FKSTATE=self.state, PATH=self.bin + ":" + os.environ["PATH"],
                 FK_GITHUB_API="http://127.0.0.1:%d" % self.port, FK_FDROID_API="http://127.0.0.1:%d/fdroid-api" % self.port,
                 FK_FDROID_REPO="http://127.0.0.1:%d/fdroid-repo" % self.port)
        for k in ("FK_NO_ROOT", "FK_PROBE_GUI", "FK_PROBE_API"):
            if k not in extra: e.pop(k, None)
        p = subprocess.run([sys.executable, SCRIPT] + list(args), env=e, capture_output=True, text=True, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=120)
        return p.returncode, p.stdout + p.stderr
    def installed(self): return sorted(l.split()[0] for l in open(self.state + "/packages").read().splitlines() if l.strip())
    def close(self): self.srv.shutdown(); shutil.rmtree(self.tmp, ignore_errors=True)

class Fingerprints(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_all_schemes(self):
        e = Env(); self.addCleanup(e.close); k = e.key("A")
        for scheme in ("v1", "v2", "both"):
            p = "%s/%s.apk" % (e.tmp, scheme); make_apk(p, "x", k, scheme)
            self.assertEqual(tp.apk_certs(p), [k.fp], scheme)
            self.assertEqual(subprocess.run(["openssl", "x509", "-in", e.tmp + "/A.pem", "-noout", "-fingerprint", "-sha256"], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip().split("=")[1].replace(":", "").lower(), k.fp)
        make_apk(e.tmp + "/u.apk", "x", None); self.assertEqual(tp.apk_certs(e.tmp + "/u.apk"), [])
        open(e.tmp + "/junk.apk", "wb").write(b"not a zip"); self.assertEqual(tp.apk_certs(e.tmp + "/junk.apk"), [])
        self.assertTrue(tp.same_signer([k.fp], [k.fp, "x"])); self.assertFalse(tp.same_signer([k.fp], ["y"])); self.assertFalse(tp.same_signer([], [k.fp]))

class Flow(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self): self.e = Env(); self.addCleanup(self.e.close)
    def both_github(self, key, digest=None):
        self.e.github("termux/termux-gui", [("gui-x86.apk", key, digest), ("gui-arm64-v8a.apk", key, digest)]); self.e.github("termux/termux-api", [("api.apk", key, digest)])
    def both_fdroid(self, key): self.e.fdroid("com.termux.gui", key); self.e.fdroid("com.termux.api", key)
    def test_github_source_matches(self):
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", "K1"); self.both_github("K1"); self.both_fdroid("K2")
        rc, out = e.run(); self.assertEqual(rc, 0, out); self.assertEqual(e.installed(), ["com.termux", "com.termux.api", "com.termux.gui"])
        self.assertFalse([r for r in e.requests if "fdroid" in r], "F-Droid not needed"); self.assertIn("signature matches Termux", out)
        self.assertTrue([r for r in e.requests if "gui-arm64-v8a" in r], "picked the build for this CPU")
        import glob; logs = glob.glob(e.home + "/.flashkit/plugin-install-*.log"); self.assertTrue(logs and all("DONE" in open(l).read() for l in logs))
        self.assertIn("com.termux.app.TermuxActivity", open(e.state + "/am.log").read())
        self.assertIn("com.termux.gui SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW allow", open(e.state + "/appops.log").read(), "overlay permission granted automatically")
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(e.home + "/.flashkit/open-next"), "no restart happened, so no restart marker is left")
    def test_fdroid_termux_tries_fdroid_first(self):
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", "K2", installer="org.fdroid.fdroid"); self.both_github("K1"); self.both_fdroid("K2")
        rc, out = e.run(); self.assertEqual(rc, 0, out); self.assertFalse([r for r in e.requests if r.startswith("/dl/") or "/repos/" in r], "GitHub not needed")
    def test_falls_back_to_other_source(self):
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", "K2", scheme="v2"); self.both_github("K1"); self.both_fdroid("K2")
        rc, out = e.run(); self.assertEqual(rc, 0, out); self.assertIn("signed by a different key", out); self.assertIn("com.termux.gui", e.installed())
    def test_no_matching_download(self):
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", "K3"); self.both_github("K1"); self.both_fdroid("K2")
        rc, out = e.run(); self.assertEqual(rc, 2, out); self.assertIn("SAME place", out); self.assertEqual(e.installed(), ["com.termux"])
    def test_bad_checksum_is_skipped(self):
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", "K2"); self.both_github("K2", digest="sha256:" + "0" * 64); self.both_fdroid("K2")
        rc, out = e.run(); self.assertEqual(rc, 0, out); self.assertIn("checksum does not match", out)
    def test_wrong_signature_plugin_is_replaced(self):
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", "K1"); e.install_pkg("com.termux.gui", "K9"); e.install_pkg("com.termux.api", "K1"); self.both_github("K1")
        rc, out = e.run("--check"); self.assertEqual(rc, 3); self.assertIn("DIFFERENT key", out)
        rc, out = e.run("--yes"); self.assertEqual(rc, 0, out)
        self.assertIn("com.termux.gui", open(e.state + "/uninstalled.log").read()); self.assertEqual(open(e.state + "/installed.log").read().split(), ["com.termux.gui"])
        self.assertEqual(e.run("--check")[0], 0)
    def test_nothing_to_do_makes_no_requests(self):
        e = self.e
        for p in ("com.termux", "com.termux.gui", "com.termux.api"): e.install_pkg(p, "K1")
        rc, out = e.run(); self.assertEqual((rc, e.requests), (0, [])); self.assertIn("Nothing to do", out)
    def test_unreadable_termux_signature_still_installs(self):
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", None, real_key="K1"); self.both_github("K1")
        rc, out = e.run(); self.assertEqual(rc, 0, out); self.assertIn("can't be read", out)
    def test_blind_pm_without_root_is_not_fooled(self):
        """Android hides other apps from a normal Termux user - the installed plugins must still be found (through root)."""
        e = self.e
        for p in ("com.termux", "com.termux.gui", "com.termux.api"): e.install_pkg(p, "K1")
        open(e.state + "/blind", "w").close(); self.both_github("K9")
        rc, out = e.run(); self.assertEqual((rc, e.requests), (0, [])); self.assertIn("Nothing to do", out); self.assertIn("signature matches", out)
    def test_working_plugin_counts_as_installed_even_if_hidden(self):
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", "K1"); open(e.state + "/packages", "w").write("com.termux null\n"); open(e.state + "/blind", "w").close()
        rc, out = e.run(FK_PROBE_GUI="true", FK_PROBE_API="true", FK_NO_ROOT="1")
        self.assertEqual((rc, e.requests), (0, [])); self.assertIn("Nothing to do", out)
    def test_android_refuses_first_source_so_next_is_tried(self):
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", None, real_key="K2"); self.both_github("K1"); self.both_fdroid("K2")
        rc, out = e.run(); self.assertEqual(rc, 0, out); self.assertIn("Android refused it", out)
        self.assertEqual(e.installed(), ["com.termux", "com.termux.api", "com.termux.gui"]); self.assertTrue([r for r in e.requests if "fdroid-repo" in r])
    def test_existing_working_plugin_is_never_overwritten(self):
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", None, real_key="K2"); e.install_pkg("com.termux.gui", "K2"); e.install_pkg("com.termux.api", "K2"); self.both_github("K1")
        rc, out = e.run(); self.assertEqual((rc, e.requests), (0, [])); self.assertIn("Nothing to do", out)
    def test_no_root_opens_the_android_installer(self):
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", "K1"); self.both_github("K1")
        rc, out = e.run(FK_NO_ROOT="1"); self.assertEqual(rc, 0, out); self.assertIn("No root", out); self.assertIn("tap Install", out)
        self.assertEqual(len(open(e.state + "/open.log").read().split()), 2); self.assertEqual(sorted(open(e.state + "/installed.log").read().split()), ["com.termux.api", "com.termux.gui"])
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(e.state + "/tmp"), "nothing went through su")
    def test_no_root_and_blind_pm_uses_the_plugins_answer(self):
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", "K1"); open(e.state + "/blind", "w").close(); self.both_github("K1")
        rc, out = e.run(FK_NO_ROOT="1", FK_PROBE_GUI='test -f "$FKSTATE/apks/com.termux.gui.apk"', FK_PROBE_API='test -f "$FKSTATE/apks/com.termux.api.apk"')
        self.assertEqual(rc, 0, out); self.assertIn("Termux:GUI installed", out)
    def test_unwritable_log_left_by_an_older_run_is_not_a_problem(self):
        """Your phone's crash: an older run (as root) left plugin-install.log that the Termux user can't open for writing."""
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", "K1"); self.both_github("K1")
        os.makedirs(e.home + "/.flashkit"); old = e.home + "/.flashkit/plugin-install.log"; open(old, "w").write("old"); os.chmod(old, 0o444)
        rc, out = e.run(); self.assertEqual(rc, 0, out); self.assertNotIn("Traceback", out); self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(old), "old log cleaned up")
    def test_offline_is_reported_not_crashed(self):
        e = self.e; e.install_pkg("com.termux", "K1"); rc, out = e.run()
        self.assertEqual(rc, 2); self.assertIn("not available there", out); self.assertIn("no internet", out); self.assertNotIn("Traceback", out)

if __name__ == "__main__": unittest.main(verbosity=1)
```

`tools/gen_icons.py`:

```py
"""Draws the Material-3-Expressive-style icon tiles and nav icons (offline, PIL, 4x supersampled)."""
import math, base64, io, json
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

SS = 4
# palette: container / on-container (dark M3 tonal roles)
KINDS = {
    "primary":   ("#33477A", "#DCE5FF"),
    "secondary": ("#3D4659", "#DDE3F4"),
    "tertiary":  ("#54406A", "#F0DEFF"),
    "danger":    ("#6B2A29", "#FFDAD6"),
    "neutral":   ("#2A303C", "#C8CEDC"),
}
NAV_PILL = "#3A4A74"; NAV_ON = "#DCE5FF"; NAV_OFF = "#A3ABBB"

def rgba(h, a=255):
    h = h.lstrip("#"); return (int(h[0:2], 16), int(h[2:4], 16), int(h[4:6], 16), a)

class Pen:
    """Draw in a 96x96 design space onto a (w x h) canvas, centred at (cx, cy) design units."""
    def __init__(self, img, scale, ox=0, oy=0):
        self.d = ImageDraw.Draw(img); self.k = scale; self.ox = ox; self.oy = oy
    def P(self, x, y): return (self.ox + x * self.k, self.oy + y * self.k)
    def line(self, pts, w, col):
        pp = [self.P(*p) for p in pts]; W = w * self.k
        self.d.line(pp, fill=col, width=int(round(W)), joint="curve")
        r = W / 2
        for (x, y) in (pp[0], pp[-1]):
            self.d.ellipse([x - r, y - r, x + r, y + r], fill=col)
    def poly(self, pts, col): self.d.polygon([self.P(*p) for p in pts], fill=col)
    def rrect(self, x0, y0, x1, y1, r, col=None, outline=None, w=0):
        a, b = self.P(x0, y0), self.P(x1, y1)
        self.d.rounded_rectangle([a, b], radius=r * self.k, fill=col, outline=outline, width=int(round(w * self.k)))
    def circle(self, cx, cy, r, col=None, outline=None, w=0):
        x, y = self.P(cx, cy); R = r * self.k
        self.d.ellipse([x - R, y - R, x + R, y + R], fill=col, outline=outline, width=int(round(w * self.k)))
    def arc(self, cx, cy, r, a0, a1, w, col):
        x, y = self.P(cx, cy); R = r * self.k; W = w * self.k
        self.d.arc([x - R, y - R, x + R, y + R], a0, a1, fill=col, width=int(round(W)))
        for a in (a0, a1):
            ex, ey = x + R * math.cos(math.radians(a)), y + R * math.sin(math.radians(a))
            self.d.ellipse([ex - W / 2, ey - W / 2, ex + W / 2, ey + W / 2], fill=col)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- glyphs (design space 96x96, glyph ~48 wide)
def g_sideload(p, c):      # up arrow into a tray
    p.line([(30, 56), (30, 68), (66, 68), (66, 56)], 7, c)
    p.line([(48, 60), (48, 26)], 7, c)
    p.line([(35, 39), (48, 26), (61, 39)], 7, c)
def g_flash(p, c):         # lightning bolt
    p.poly([(54, 22), (32, 52), (45, 52), (40, 74), (64, 42), (50, 42)], c)
def g_apk(p, c):           # package / box
    p.poly([(48, 24), (68, 35), (68, 59), (48, 70), (28, 59), (28, 35)], None) if False else None
    p.line([(48, 24), (68, 35), (68, 59), (48, 70), (28, 59), (28, 35), (48, 24)], 6, c)
    p.line([(28, 35), (48, 46), (68, 35)], 6, c)
    p.line([(48, 46), (48, 70)], 6, c)
def g_reboot(p, c):        # power
    p.arc(48, 52, 20, -60, 240, 7, c)
    p.line([(48, 24), (48, 48)], 7, c)
def g_erase(p, c):         # trash can
    p.line([(30, 33), (66, 33)], 7, c)
    p.line([(41, 33), (41, 27), (55, 27), (55, 33)], 6, c)
    p.line([(35, 40), (38, 68), (58, 68), (61, 40)], 7, c)
    p.line([(46, 47), (46, 61)], 5, c); p.line([(52, 47), (52, 61)], 5, c)
def g_gsi(p, c):           # layers
    for dy, w in ((0, 7), (14, 7), (28, 7)):
        pass
    p.line([(48, 24), (70, 36), (48, 48), (26, 36), (48, 24)], 6, c)
    p.line([(26, 47), (48, 59), (70, 47)], 6, c)
    p.line([(26, 58), (48, 70), (70, 58)], 6, c)
def g_recovery(p, c):      # wrench: open-ended head (gap facing up-right) + handle
    p.line([(55, 41), (30, 66)], 9, c)
    p.arc(59, 37, 13, 350, 640, 9, c)
    p.circle(30, 66, 3.2, col=(0, 0, 0, 0)) if False else None
def g_refresh(p, c):       # circular arrow, head at the end of the arc
    cx, cy, r = 48, 49, 19
    p.arc(cx, cy, r, 25, 315, 7, c)
    th = math.radians(315)
    ex, ey = cx + r * math.cos(th), cy + r * math.sin(th)
    tx, ty = -math.sin(th), math.cos(th)          # clockwise tangent
    nx, ny = math.cos(th), math.sin(th)           # outward normal
    tip = (ex + tx * 13, ey + ty * 13)
    p.poly([tip, (ex + nx * 11, ey + ny * 11), (ex - nx * 11, ey - ny * 11)], c)
def g_fix(p, c):           # plug
    p.rrect(35, 38, 61, 62, 6, col=None, outline=c, w=6)
    p.line([(42, 38), (42, 26)], 6, c); p.line([(54, 38), (54, 26)], 6, c)
    p.line([(48, 62), (48, 74)], 6, c)
# nav
def g_home(p, c):
    p.line([(26, 48), (48, 28), (70, 48)], 7, c)
    p.line([(32, 44), (32, 68), (64, 68), (64, 44)], 7, c)
    p.line([(44, 68), (44, 54), (52, 54), (52, 68)], 6, c)
def g_folder(p, c):
    p.line([(26, 36), (26, 66), (70, 66), (70, 42), (50, 42), (45, 34), (30, 34), (26, 36)], 6, c)
def g_terminal(p, c):
    p.rrect(24, 30, 72, 66, 9, col=None, outline=c, w=6)
    p.line([(35, 41), (44, 48), (35, 55)], 6, c)
    p.line([(49, 56), (60, 56)], 6, c)
def g_gear(p, c):
    p.circle(48, 48, 19, col=c)
    for i in range(8):
        a = math.radians(i * 45)
        x, y = 48 + 22 * math.cos(a), 48 + 22 * math.sin(a)
        p.circle(x, y, 5, col=c)
    p.circle(48, 48, 8.5, col=(0, 0, 0, 0))   # punched hole
GLYPHS = {"sideload": g_sideload, "flash": g_flash, "apk": g_apk, "reboot": g_reboot, "erase": g_erase,
          "gsi": g_gsi, "recovery": g_recovery, "refresh": g_refresh, "fix": g_fix}
def g_storage(p, c):       # a drive: rounded box, a line and a dot
    p.rrect(22, 36, 74, 64, 9, col=None, outline=c, w=6)
    p.line([(32, 50), (50, 50)], 6, c)
    p.circle(63, 50, 3.6, col=c)
NAV = {"home": g_home, "files": g_folder, "storage": g_storage, "console": g_terminal, "settings": g_gear}
TILE_KIND = {"sideload": "primary", "flash": "primary", "apk": "secondary", "reboot": "secondary", "erase": "danger",
             "gsi": "tertiary", "recovery": "tertiary", "refresh": "neutral", "fix": "neutral"}

def punch(img):
    """gear hole: pixels painted fully transparent need replace-not-blend; handled by drawing on a mask copy."""
    return img

def tile(name, size=192):
    cont, on = KINDS[TILE_KIND[name]]
    img = Image.new("RGBA", (size * SS, size * SS), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    p = Pen(img, size * SS / 96.0)
    p.rrect(2, 2, 94, 94, 34, col=rgba(cont))
    GLYPHS[name](p, rgba(on))
    return img.resize((size, size), Image.LANCZOS)

def nav(name, selected, w=192, h=96):
    img = Image.new("RGBA", (w * SS, h * SS), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    k = h * SS / 96.0
    if selected:
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
        d.rounded_rectangle([2 * k, 8 * k, (w * SS / k - 2) * k, 88 * k], radius=40 * k, fill=rgba(NAV_PILL))
    glyph = Image.new("RGBA", (w * SS, h * SS), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    p = Pen(glyph, k, ox=(w * SS - 96 * k) / 2, oy=0)
    p_scale_fix = p
    NAV[name](p, rgba(NAV_ON if selected else NAV_OFF))
    if name == "settings":                       # punch the gear hole out of the glyph layer
        hole = Image.new("L", glyph.size, 255); hd = ImageDraw.Draw(hole)
        cx, cy = p.P(48, 48); R = 8.5 * k
        hd.ellipse([cx - R, cy - R, cx + R, cy + R], fill=0)
        glyph.putalpha(Image.composite(glyph.getchannel("A"), Image.new("L", glyph.size, 0), hole))
    img = Image.alpha_composite(img, glyph)
    return img.resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)

CAP = {"primary": "#2D4275", "tonal": "#2A303C", "danger": "#5F1A17", "flat": "#1B1F27"}

def cap(kind, side, h=168):
    w = h // 2
    img = Image.new("RGBA", (w * SS, h * SS), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
    col = rgba(CAP[kind])
    R = h * SS / 2
    if side == "L":
        d.ellipse([0, 0, 2 * R, h * SS - 1], fill=col)
        d.rectangle([R, 0, w * SS, h * SS], fill=col)
    else:
        d.ellipse([w * SS - 2 * R, 0, w * SS, h * SS - 1], fill=col)
        d.rectangle([0, 0, w * SS - R, h * SS], fill=col)
    return img.resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)

def flat_png(im, rgb_hex, levels=16):
    """One exact colour + a 16-step alpha ramp (palette PNG with tRNS): tiny, and no colour seam."""
    a = im.getchannel("A")
    idx = a.point(lambda v: int(round(v / 255.0 * (levels - 1))))
    p = Image.new("P", im.size)
    r, g, b = int(rgb_hex[1:3], 16), int(rgb_hex[3:5], 16), int(rgb_hex[5:7], 16)
    p.putpalette([r, g, b] * levels + [0] * (3 * (256 - levels)))
    p.putdata(list(idx.getdata()))
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    p.save(buf, "PNG", optimize=True, transparency=bytes(int(round(i * 255.0 / (levels - 1))) for i in range(levels)))
    return buf.getvalue()

def small(im, colors=48):
    """palette-quantise (keeps alpha) - the art is flat colour, this cuts the size to a third."""
    return im.quantize(colors=colors, method=Image.Quantize.FASTOCTREE, dither=Image.Dither.NONE)

def png_bytes(im):
    b = io.BytesIO(); im.save(b, "PNG", optimize=True); return b.getvalue()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    out = {}
    for n in GLYPHS: out["tile_" + n] = png_bytes(small(tile(n, 192)))
    for n in NAV:
        out["nav_" + n] = png_bytes(small(nav(n, False, 192, 96), 24))
        out["nav_%s_sel" % n] = png_bytes(small(nav(n, True, 192, 96), 24))
    for k in CAP:
        out["cap_%s_L" % k] = flat_png(cap(k, "L"), CAP[k]); out["cap_%s_R" % k] = flat_png(cap(k, "R"), CAP[k])
    json.dump({k: base64.b64encode(v).decode() for k, v in out.items()}, open("icons.json", "w"))
    print({k: len(v) for k, v in out.items()}); print("total raw bytes:", sum(len(v) for v in out.values()))
    bg = rgba("#101318")
    sheet = Image.new("RGBA", (10 + 5 * 202, 10 + 2 * 202 + 20 + 2 * 110 + 20 + 100), bg)
    for i, n in enumerate(GLYPHS):
        t = Image.open(io.BytesIO(out["tile_" + n])).convert("RGBA"); sheet.alpha_composite(t, (10 + (i % 5) * 202, 10 + (i // 5) * 202))
    y0 = 10 + 2 * 202 + 20
    for i, n in enumerate(NAV):
        for j, sel in enumerate((False, True)):
            t = Image.open(io.BytesIO(out["nav_%s%s" % (n, "_sel" if sel else "")])).convert("RGBA")
            sheet.alpha_composite(t, (10 + i * 250, y0 + j * 110))
    # a demo pill built from the real parts: cap + colour block + cap
    y1 = y0 + 2 * 110 + 20
    x = 10
    for kind in CAP:
        L = Image.open(io.BytesIO(out["cap_%s_L" % kind])).convert("RGBA"); R = Image.open(io.BytesIO(out["cap_%s_R" % kind])).convert("RGBA")
        mid = Image.new("RGBA", (150, 168 // 1), rgba(CAP[kind]))
        pill = Image.new("RGBA", (L.width + 150 + R.width, 168), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        pill.alpha_composite(L, (0, 0)); pill.alpha_composite(mid, (L.width, 0)); pill.alpha_composite(R, (L.width + 150, 0))
        pill = pill.resize((pill.width * 100 // 168, 100), Image.LANCZOS)
        sheet.alpha_composite(pill, (x, y1)); x += pill.width + 14
    sheet.convert("RGB").save("preview.png")
```
